# Causal transfer from esse to particular perfections

This investigation continues merged #731 from main
`aab5dcf4827b03dcc18be2c2b115990de0fcf7cc`, targeting
`STMT-CAUSE-OF-BEING-CAUSES-EVERY-PERFECTION`. The finding is a strict scope
reduction with three conditional applications and a surviving substantive
rival under a specified shared background. It is **not** a discharge of causal
transfer. Legacy Challenges, Solutions and the designated root route are kept.
The existing current handoff is in `docs/proposition-structure.md`.

The current cluster-level judgment is the
[2026-11-18 assessment](#current-cluster-assessment-under-the-substantive-objection-standard-2026-11-18);
the sections below it keep their own dates and wording.

The [particular-actuality investigation](actuality-esse-research.md#9-does-a-particular-actuality-share-in-its-bearers-received-being) tests whether a real mode draws on the received being of its subject, and what further premise would attribute causal responsibility for the exercise to that giver.

The [later reading of Durand and Suárez](actuality-esse-research.md#12-what-the-conservationist-grants-about-this-effect) settles the historical question left open below. Durand attributes the very action mediately to the giver of its exercised power. His denial of immediate divine concurrence leaves the earlier finite skill comparison in its role as an analyst’s control.

The [acquired-quality and relational tests](actuality-esse-research.md#13-who-causes-an-acquired-quality-or-a-relation-to-obtain) examine causal histories that can carry mediated responsibility to a particular respect, including when the other relatum changes. They give a conditional route to the existing universal transfer premise.

No new source claim is introduced. Existing q.4/q.6 support claims remain
support, not assertions of the legacy or narrower universal formula. A sentence
about all modes of being is not automatically a theorem quantifying over the
current `Respect` carrier. In particular a route using q.44 to treat each
actuality as an effect would need a justified representation of particular
actualities as beings/effects and coverage of secondary operation; neither is
provided by `ActualityOnlyInsofarAsBeingReading`.

## Causal meaning and the strongest case

`CausesIn c x r` means effective causal responsibility for x's actuality r. At
esse it concerns x's being, not merely x's coming to be. At another respect it
concerns that actuality in x, not merely c's possessing it. The predicate can
include a primary cause working through a real secondary cause when that
attribution is justified. It does not mean exclusive, sufficient, deterministic,
or temporally earlier cause; no such properties follow from the parameter.

A builder originates a house's arrangement without continuously maintaining
its existence. The continued condition of its materials is a different
explanatory question (q.104). A parent/child example alone therefore cannot
refute transfer from **sustaining** esse. A continuous supply sustaining an
organism or device can be necessary for its operation while the specific act
is selected and produced by an agent or mechanism. This latter example motivates
the rival's distinction, but is not identified with Aquinas's universal
existential cause: ordinary supplies do not give subsistent being to every
creature. A guard removing a threat illustrates mere enablement/indirect
preservation, explicitly distinguished in q.104. A craftsman using an axe
illustrates ordered active contribution (q.105); the craftsman need not create
the axe. These analogies constrain meanings rather than prove metaphysical
universality.

The strongest available positive argument is participation plus concurrence:
all positive actuality is an exercise or determination of being; creaturely
forms and powers participate in being; the primary source gives and sustains
those forms/powers and actively applies secondary agents to operation; thus
primary causal responsibility can include the particular exercise without
excluding the secondary cause. Q.105's different-order explanation directly
answers the objection that a complete primary cause would make secondary
causation superfluous. Primary and secondary responsibility can both be true
in different orders of effective causation.

**OPEN:** dependence of the bearer and its powers is not by itself active
contribution to each exercise. The extra application/concurrence step needs
independent defense. Also open is the identification of a universal esse source
with the source's subsistent/first agent. Inferring either from already named
God's powers and then using it to name that same witness would be circular.
No such premise is used in the new naming proof. The positive argument is
preserved as sourced reasoning and precise missing steps, not falsely certified
as a consequence of existence dependence alone.

## Scope and structural properties

The legacy formula is `∀ c x r, CausesIn c x esse → InAct x r → CausesIn c x r`.
It includes nonuniversal sources and self-effects. The new open statement
`STMT-UNIVERSAL-CAUSE-TRANSFERS-PERFECTIONS` restricts c by the **existing**
`CausesAllInGenus InAct CausesIn esse c` and restricts x to x ≠ c. It retains
the esse antecedent, so the comparison does not require actuality-to-being.
The scope condition says nothing about other perfections, God, maximality,
being uncaused, or `IsFirstBeing PriorSimply`; it is independently
characterized existential coverage, not the desired conclusion renamed.
It is not a complete definition of Aquinas's first/universal efficient cause.

In naming, the retained `MaximumInGenusCausesAllInGenusReading` at esse supplies
this scope condition at the supplied maximum **before** naming. In the reduced
nominal description it is already an explicit condition on the supplied m.
In goodness it follows from the separately retained God-to-maximality and
genus premises; this post-naming use is not fed back into the naming proof.
No maximum, God, or first mover exists merely because these universal
conditional applications are proved.

No transitivity, global asymmetry, well-foundedness, or temporal/modal
necessitation law is added to `CausesIn`. The existing strict causal-ascent
and genus principles are retained as **open**, nonlogical background. Numerical
ranks in the finite models independently justify their strict comparisons;
this justifies the models' order, not real causes' strict ascent. No
same-respect causal cycle is present in either table. Cross-respect dependence
is allowed by the tested vocabulary and is explicitly present in the rival:
0 causes 2's esse, while 2 causes 0's skill. Absence of a distinct incoming
esse cause does not rule out incoming causes in another respect or establish
priority across every causal order. If a stronger notion of first cause excludes
this, that additional condition and its independent applicability are required.

## Shared-background tests and surviving rival

Both tables use `Being = TransferRole` (four distinct constructors), `Respect = Bool`, false = esse and true = skill.
All four beings have both actualities. Role 0 is the universal existential
source, 1 a particular existential supporter, 2 a secondary producer, and 3
the recipient. Role 1 is **not** identified with a past generator: a generating
parent is an analogy to reject, not a sustaining edge smuggled into the model.
These are schematic causal roles, not a purported physical implementation.

| Causal respect | Concurrent table | Secondary-only rival |
| --- | --- | --- |
| esse | 0 → 1,2,3; 1 → 3 | identical |
| skill | 0 → 1,2,3; 2 → 3 | 2 → 0,1,3 |
| Strict rank in esse | 0 > 1 > 2 > 3 | identical |
| Strict rank in skill | 0 > 1 > 2 > 3 | 2 > 0 > 1 > 3 |

Each jointly satisfies `TransferModelBackground`: genus at **both** respects;
the legacy strict cause-to-more principle; actuality-to-being; the existing
formal/eminent preexistence principle; maximum 0 at esse; universal esse
causation by 0; no distinct incoming esse cause to 0; actual particular-source
and secondary-producer edges. Carriers and actuality extensions are populated;
universal-source transfer is tested at actual edges, not vacuously. Both
comparisons are under the identical background formula, evaluated at their
explicit tables. There are no worlds/accessibility parameters or modal claims.

The concurrent table satisfies restricted transfer but falsifies unrestricted
transfer at (1,3,skill). The secondary-only table falsifies even restricted
transfer at (0,3,skill). Since **possession** of both perfections holds for all
beings and preexistence holds, adding that existing premise does not repair
the inference. The rival grants existential dependence but denies its
sufficiency for attributing each particular causal exercise to its source.
It does not deny the source's own actuality or the need for a real secondary
producer. It can accept sustaining and producing in different senses, while
rejecting their conflation in a single transfer rule.

These are conditional interpretive controls, not proof that a conservationist
metaphysics is possible, not an attribution to Durandus/Papineau/Hume, and not
models of every premise in the root case. In particular they do not witness
full-description naming, the God predicate, firstness in `PriorSimply`, pure
act, or the historical/revelation premises. The additional burden of extending
an ordinary preserving/producing distinction to an ultimate source stays open.

Reuse `MaximeEnsIsGodViaFullDescription.withoutPerfectionsBridgeNotGod` only as
its original omission control and
`FirstClosure.causeOfBeingCausesEveryPerfection_notLogical` only as failure of
logical validity over unconstrained parameters. They are not whole-root or
interpretation-grounded rival models. The new tests add the explicit shared
causal background and active, inhabited roles those controls do not claim.

## Source defense audit: why would an esse maximum cause other beings? (2026-10-31)

This bounded source lane targets **STMT-MAXIMUM-IN-GENUS-CAUSES-ALL-IN-GENUS**,
not the distinct transfer from an already given esse cause to particular
perfections. Its dossier and existing attributed/supporting claims were
inspected. The existing primary witnesses **AQUINAS-ST-I-Q4-A2**,
**AQUINAS-ST-I-Q44-A1**, **AQUINAS-ST-I-Q3-A4** and **AQUINAS-ST-I-Q13-A5**
were materialized and read in full, including objections/replies. These are the
pinned SCTA/Leonine Latin, revision
`f9065dc8ce7bc790b8c972e49ee19d661f028be6`; exact witness paths/blob hashes remain
in their unchanged passage manifests. **AQUINAS-SCG-I-13-EN** was materialized;
its relevant final argument context is I.13[28]–[35], especially[33]–[34], in the
existing Pegis1955 translation snapshot `aquinas-summa-contra-gentiles-en.json`.
No translation bytes or source claims were added or changed, and no unread SCG
chapter is cited as evidence.

**The strongest positive argument is participation in received esse, not bare
comparative maximality.** Q.3 a.4 co., first proof, states a local conditional:
if a subject's esse differs from its essence, that esse must come either from
its essential principles or from another; its essential principles alone cannot
sufficiently efficiently originate its own caused being; hence an external cause
is required. This is real causal reasoning whose general conditional does not
need an already unique God or a common cause. Its causal-exhaustiveness and
no-efficient-self-origination premises remain substantive, explicitly recorded
commitments. It excludes neither every intrinsic noncausal reason nor the
brute/noncausal rival merely by being formalized. The existing
`EssenceEsseExternalCause.distinctEsseExternalFromExhaustion` proves the
conditional as a declared transcription; it does not defend its two premises.
The local conclusion supplies *some external cause*, not the particular
maximum as that cause, and not yet an essentially-esse source.

Q.44 a.1 co. supplies the next stronger rationale: a perfection possessed by
participation is caused in its possessor by that to which it belongs essentially.
The heated-iron/fire illustration explains received possession versus essential
possession, rather than measuring the hottest object on a neutral scale. The
paragraph then explicitly appeals to previously established subsistent esse and
its numerical uniqueness, infers that all others participate in esse, and
concludes one first source of their diverse received being. Ad1 distinguishes
being itself from participated being: causal dependence need not enter the
abstract definition of being, while still following from received being.
Ad2 explicitly permits caused necessary effects; modality alone is not the
source's criterion of causal dependence. None of these steps says merely that
the largest item on a degree ordering creates every lower item.

A faithful schematic decomposition of that paragraph therefore has four
separate burdens: a designated n is subsistent/unreceived esse; every relevant
other recipient has esse by participation; received esse depends causally on
what has it essentially; and the relevant essentially-esse source is n rather
than a different whole necessary source. Q.44 uses prior subsistence and unity
to supply the recipient classification and numerical source selection. These
may be investigated independently, but cannot be silently imported when the
current task is precisely to justify universal causality or unity of an unnamed
maximum. The existing claim **AQUINAS-ST-I-Q44-A1-PARTICIPATED-ESSE-CAUSE** is
correctly weaker: participated esse requires an external cause. It should not
be rewritten as a newly established universal common-source theorem.

**Why q.4 a.2 does not discharge this interface.** In its first proof, effect
perfections preexist in the agent formally or eminently; applying that principle
to all effects explicitly presupposes God as first effective cause. Reversing
it would turn an agent's possession into causal responsibility for every other
possessor. In its second proof, subsistent esse has unrestricted fullness of
being and therefore lacks no perfection. That is a claim from subsistence to
fullness, not a derivation from the weak encoded maximum to subsistence or
universal causation. Ad1–2 allows opposed finite perfections to coexist
eminently in the one cause; a formal-character distinction cannot automatically
be treated as absence of the relevant eminent perfection. Ad3 distinguishes
having being from possessing every mode of being. Thus neither proof supplies
maximal-possession-to-production merely by removing the word God.

**Analogy preserves a causal reading but does not manufacture its coverage.**
Q.13 a.5 co. rejects univocity and pure equivocity, using a causal proportion
between medicine and animal health as its relevant analogy. The text's
creature-to-primary ordering is explicitly causal, with eminent preexistence;
it is not simple membership in a homogeneous genus. Ad1 distinguishes a
particular univocal generator from a universal nonunivocal cause; ad3 rejects
God and creatures forming one proportionate genus. The cited historical solar
illustration is not taken here as independently verified modern physics.
The already certified
`QuartaViaAttributionGenusAtBeing.attributionOrderGenusPrincipleAtBeing` exposes
its exact reconstruction: approach to a primary, strict primacy, creatures'
causal ordering to that primary, all others being creatures, and EM. It first
identifies a maximum with the supplied primary and then applies the supplied
causal ordering. It does not derive causal ordering or all-other-creature
coverage from the order alone. **STMT-OTHERS-THAN-PRIMARY-ARE-CREATURES** is
already recorded as an open unattributed coverage premise. The present reading
supplies no new evidence discharging it before the participation/uniqueness
burdens above have been met.

SCG I.13[33] separately argues for a first efficient cause through an ordered
causal series. [34] separately reaches a maximally true/being item through
comparisons approaching the true; it does not furnish a missing proof that any
weak maximum is the cause of every being, or identify these independently
introduced roles without further argument. Merely joining the two paragraphs'
God labels is not a proof that their witnesses coincide. This is a source-scope
finding, not a new criticism of the Aristotelian reference being audited in
another lane.

**Actionable positive next connection.** The local q.3 a.4 conditional can be
used for the SAME actual concrete root without assuming universal max causality:
existing-world esse actuality makes essence/esse nonidentity eligible for the
external-cause theorem; the explicit direct esse-cause-to-actual-concrete-ground
interface turns that cause into an incoming ground, contradicting rootness;
EM yields equality of the represented esse/essence. Its q.3 Profile must use
the same InAct, CausesIn and esse fields definitionally. This does not need
all-world underivedness, modal lifting, grades or genus causality. It would
supply a substantive conditional attribute of the supplied individual while
leaving the local causal-exhaustiveness/no-self-origination and correspondence
premises open. Equality alone is still not universal common causality or an
adequate intrinsic explanation. No new theorem is implemented by this audit.

Actual premise change: zero. No claim, definition or source attribution changed.
Ledger I remains independently open; the positive source route identifies its
received-being and numerical coverage prerequisites instead of hiding them in
an enriched meaning of maximum.


### Cited Aristotle direction and the weak-maximum reading

The independent adversarial source lane materialized and read full
AQUINAS-ST-I-Q2-A3, ARIST-MET-II-1, AQUINAS-IN-METAPH-II-L2 and
AQUINAS-ST-I-Q44-A1. Its bounded interpretation agrees that Aquinas explicitly
asserts the genus-causality principle in q.2 a.3. That attribution is faithful;
absence of an independent demonstration is not itself a fidelity defect.

The cited Aristotle passage, Metaphysics II/alpha.1, 993b24–26, moves from being
the cause of a synonymous quality in others to being most such; 993b26–31
applies causal priority to truth/being. The supplied passage does not derive
the converse from every maximum to universal causation. Aquinas's commentary
II lect.2 n.4 [81858] makes the cause-to-most-such direction and univocal
condition explicit. N.5 [81859] distinguishes solar heat-causing from being
hottest in that same species. Nn.6–7 [81860–81861] discuss causal principles of
truth/being. N.8 [81862] offers a different positive participation route:
composites/participants reduce as causes to what is such essentially; that
reduction still needs warrant, not mere comparative terminology.

Q.44 a.1 co. explicitly imports previously established uniqueness of subsistent
esse before concluding one first cause of other participated being. Feeding
that conclusion back into the proof of that same uniqueness would not provide
an independent justification. Ad1 targets participated being rather than bare
being. Ad2 distinguishes necessity from being uncaused; this does not identify
every historical necessity sense with absolute cross-world NecessaryAt.

There is a formal applicability sensitivity even when q.2's assertion is read
faithfully: IsMaximumIn means possession with no strictly greater bearer, not
a greatest element or total comparability. Weakening an existential conclusion
to weak maximality makes that conclusion weaker; weakening the antecedent of
`maximum → causes all` makes the universal premise stronger. Tied/incomparable
maxima are now included. A source-backed participation/cause argument or a
different stronger maximum notion would need its own explicit semantics; no
source claim, old definition or frozen Challenge is changed here.

ROOT-ESSENCE-IDENTITY implements the constructive local connection described
above. It does not need this genus principle or maximum existence. It adds no
independent defense of q.3's causal exhaustion, efficient no-self-origination,
or the actual-world cause/ground interface; its same-witness result is a
conditional source composition, not universal common causality or numerical
uniqueness.


## Subsistent infinity after same-root essence/esse identity (#354, 2026-10-31)

This bounded follow-up inspected issue#354, related results, the dossier for
**STMT-SUBSISTENT-BEING-IS-INFINITE**, the prior unreceived-esse investigation,
`InfinityEternity.lean`, and the new **ROOT-ESSENCE-IDENTITY** endpoint. The
complete existing primary passages **AQUINAS-ST-I-Q7-A1** and
**AQUINAS-ST-I-Q7-A2** were materialized and read, including all objections and
replies, with the already materialized q.3 a.4 as immediate conceptual context.
The q.7 Latin witness is SCTA/Leonine,
`http://scta.info/resource/TAca84-d1e82/cod-cnLxE4`, repository
`scta-texts/TAca84`, revision`f9065dc8ce7bc790b8c972e49ee19d661f028be6`,
path`TAca84-d1e82/cod-cnLxE4_TAca84-d1e82.xml`, blob
`9c97a592ff7802389e10a056a36338eb4ee1aba4`; a.1 and a.2 are respectively
`TAca84-d1e82-Dd1e153` and `TAca84-d1e82-Dd1e356`. No source bytes, claims or
legacy types were changed. The plurality note's earlier q.7 reading is reused;
this section adds the exact consequences for the newly available represented
identity endpoint and for the proposed typed limitation design.

**What the source positively offers.** A.1 co., beginning with the distinction
between matter being bounded by form and form by matter, separates two senses
of limitation. Form perfects matter by determining its potentiality; matter
contracts the amplitude of a received form without perfecting that form. The
latter restriction motivates infinity as formal perfection, rather than the
indeterminacy of unformed matter. The paragraph then identifies esse as most
formal and applies nonreception to the divine subsistent esse. Ad2 explicitly
excludes quantitative infinity from this application. This is substantial
semantic structure for investigating receiver-limitation; a typed proof should
preserve its direction and relevant act rather than replace it by any generic
mathematical unboundedness predicate.

A.2 co. makes the required **respect indexing** decisive. A created form that
subsists without material reception can be unlimited with respect to matter,
yet its esse is received and contracted to a determinate nature because it has
esse rather than being its esse. It is not infinite simpliciter. Hence “no
material receiver” cannot serve as unreceived esse, and no one undifferentiated
Received predicate can safely collapse formal/material reception, act of being,
and a merely represented component relation. A typed restriction witness must
specify which act is received and which receiver restricts that act's formal
amplitude. The domain's admitted restrictions must be justified; selecting only
receivers as possible restrictions and then proving every restriction is a
receiver would be definitional concealment of the proposed exhaustion premise.

**The direction relevant to the new endpoint.** In the a.2 created-subsistent-
form context, essence/esse nonidentity is the stated reason for reception and
contraction of esse. This supports the direction from the stated nonidentity
to reception **in that context**, not the converse from reception to nonidentity
needed to infer nonreception from the new equality. Logically, reversing that
conditional is not licensed. A.1's application treats divine esse as subsistent
and unreceived together, with a back-reference to previous argument; it does
not provide a reductive receiver relation on arbitrary values of the corpus's
Principle sort. A.1 ad3 distinguishes subsistent unreceived esse from received
instances without reducing the distinction to ordinary predication (God is not
stone or wood). Q.3 a.4's existing Profile contains maps for esse and essence,
actuality and efficient causation, but no reception or limitation field.
Therefore `p.esseOf n = p.essenceOf n` supplies no theorem about a newly proposed
reception relation by itself. That is a missing semantic/applicability bridge,
not another consequence to recover by simplification.

**What a useful decomposition would still have to establish.** There are at
least three separate obligations before a typed receiver argument can replace
the legacy premise: (1) explain why the same candidate's represented identity
excludes genuine receipt of its esse, retaining the real act/receiver distinction;
(2) defend that every relevant limitation of that esse's formal perfection is
through an appropriate receiving potency, rather than merely proving that some
ordinary limitations arise that way; and (3) give a faithful connection between
the typed absence of such limitation and the old supplied `Infinite` predicate.
The first is not supplied by #354's proposed receiver-exhaustion principle, and
the third cannot be omitted because `Infinite` currently has no defining body.
These are design obligations, not three newly admitted assumptions or newly
registered predicates. An independent account of limitation could make the
second a meaningful general constraint; a relation merely named LimitedBy does
not yet provide that account. In particular, neither
`RootMultiplication.HasLimitingPrinciple`'s three stipulated disjuncts nor their
absence is identified with the q.7 reception account.

The frozen legacy **SubsistentBeingIsInfiniteReading** is exactly
`∀ x, esseOf x = essenceOf x → Infinite x`. Applying it to the newly proved
same-root equality is a valid conditional application, but it would retain the
very open premise under investigation, and would not discharge #354. Likewise,
defining a new word for absence of receiver-limitation does not prove the old
primitive Infinite parameter. Preserving the legacy interface while revealing
its missing semantics is more informative than another wrapper theorem.

**Disposition.** No proof file, new relation framework, model or philosophical
premise was added. The positive source finding is the distinction of perfecting
material determination from contracting formal reception and the explicit
a.2 separation of immaterial-form infinity from esse infinity. The new root
identity is a concrete prerequisite for the investigation, but a source-faithful
identity-to-nonreception bridge, receiver-exhaustion defense and old-Infinite
semantic adapter remain unresolved. No independent modal identity law,
numerical unity, God naming or root-premise discharge follows. Exact premise
change from this audit: zero. A subsequent implementation should wait until
these obligations have content that improves the actual route, rather than
renaming the old equality-to-Infinite implication.


### Reception, real constitution and actualized receptive potency (2026-10-31)

A further source test asks whether the SAME root's noncomposition could exclude
esse reception without inferring nonreception directly from equality of the q3
maps. Existing reception findings in `necessary-simple-plurality.md` and the
four-composition-senses audit were read first. The full existing primary
**AQUINAS-DE-ENTE-C3-4** witness and complete **AQUINAS-ST-I-Q3-A7** were then
materialized and read. The De ente witness is the Corpus Thomisticum Baur/Koch
chapter numbering, capp.3–4, anchors**[69874]–[69875]**, pinned source URL
`https://www.corpusthomisticum.org/oee.html`, response SHA256
`4fbd17e8136d920cd929f7b67fca45693f15eccdf4db7495bd16f9bcca119bb7`, selected
text SHA256`eb7b620a51a79ede558a09af15b41264707a14cf6f6e7af009547a20ebf78114`.
The unchanged `aquinas-de-ente-c3-4.json` manifest retains encoding and selectors.
Q.3 a.7 is the existing SCTA/Leonine q.3 snapshot at revision
`f9065dc8ce7bc790b8c972e49ee19d661f028be6`, blob
`9204053aec597c5ea95b77543135e641ce4cfdeb`, XML section
`TAca84-d1e61-Dd1e1553`. No source claim was introduced.

**Positive explicit passage.** In De ente c.3[69874], after the paragraphs on
essence/esse distinction, external efficient causation and reduction to esse
tantum, the paragraph beginning with the general assertion that whatever
receives something from another is in potency with respect to it identifies
the received item as the recipient's act. Aquinas applies this to an intellectual
substance's quiddity receiving esse. He then distinguishes this act/potency
constitution from matter/form constitution, limits the vocabulary's univocity
between intellectual and bodily cases, and in the following explanation relates
the quiddity/intelligence to that-which-is and its received esse to that-by-which
it subsists. This is an explicit account of received esse as constitutive
act/potency, not an inference from the occurrence of two words. C.4[69875],
the second mode of having essence (created intellectual substances), adds that
the received esse is limited to the receiving nature's capacity while the
quiddity remains unreceived in matter. These passages supply a stronger positive
basis for investigating reception-to-real-constitution than q.7's analogy alone.

**The actualized-potency distinction is indispensable.** Here the recipient is
a presently subsisting intelligence, with received esse as its act, yet the
quiddity is potential *with respect to that received esse*. This is receptive
potency in an already actualized constitution. It cannot be silently assigned
to the corpus's legacy `InPotency n esse`, whose current gloss is able to have
the respect without actually having it and whose same-respect non-simultaneity
is imposed alongside `InAct`. The existing-world interface simultaneously gives
`InAct n esse`. Applying the old not-both law to the recipient qua receptive
principle and to the subject qua actually existent would collapse these
respects/roles and spuriously exclude the ordinary actual received beings the
source is describing. The source's general receiving-from-another-to-potency
conditional is a sourced reading to investigate; its applicability to a
particular actual receipt is not established by calling that candidate a
receiver. No new direct receipt-to-legacy-potency bridge is adopted here.

**What noncomposition can and cannot do.** If an independently specified esse
receipt really constitutes its bearer from an actualizing act and a receiving
potential principle, and that constitution falls under the SAME `Composite`
excluded for the root, noncomposition rules it out without needing the legacy
same-respect not-both law. This is a promising choice of proof route. Its
philosophical work is to establish the genuine constitutive distinction and
map that constitution into the existing Composite predicate, not merely to
name a relation Reception. The already recorded
**STMT-MATTER-FORM-AND-ESSE-ESSENCE-ARE-COMPOSITIONS** supplies only two inclusion
clauses: matter/form constitution implies Composite; represented esse/essence
nonidentity implies Composite. It contains no reception relation and supplies
no converse. Q.3 a.7 co., fourth proof, says a composite has act and potency
(one constituent actualizes another, or parts are potential relative to the
whole); this direction alone does not prove that every pair of conceptual
act/potency descriptions is a real composite.

There is a further source-order restriction on treating the De ente passage
as an independent exclusion. C.3 has already established the essence/esse
nonidentity of the intellectual substances under discussion before deriving
their reception and explaining its act/potency constitution. Its concrete
application therefore does not independently prove, against an arbitrary
proposed identity-bearer, that ANY alleged receipt must introduce a real
distinction. The general receiving-from-another conditional supports the
receptive-role analysis; a universal receipt-to-real-composition rule for the
new candidate still needs faithful semantics and defense. C.4's later
whole-quiddity account of genus/differentia reinforces the limit: conceptual
classification of an immaterial nature is not by itself two real constituents.
Nor is receipt *from another* automatically identical to every use of receipt
*in something* in the q.7 limitation argument; causal origin and internal
receiving constitution must be kept distinct until their connection is argued.

**Bounded result.** The positive finding changes the design: test actualized
receptive constitution against noncomposition, rather than using ordinary
unactualized potency plus not-both to manufacture a contradiction. It does not
yet remove an independent burden. The missing identity-to-nonreception step
could be replaced by a reception-to-real-constitution applicability step, but
has not been discharged. Receiver-exhaustion for all relevant limitations and
the old primitive-Infinite semantic adapter remain separate obligations.
No new framework, source claim, proof file, model or philosophical premise was
added; no modal invariance or numerical unity follows from this reading.

### Typed reception derivation (#354, 2026-11-05)

SUBSISTENT-INFINITY-FROM-RECEPTION takes the three obligations above as five
explicit open premises. They use one esse-indexed reception relation
`EsseReceivedIn x r`, one `EsseLimited x`, and a receptive potency
`ReceptivePotencyTo p a` that is kept apart from the legacy `InPotency`
(DEF-ESSE-RECEIVED-IN, DEF-ESSE-LIMITED, DEF-RECEPTIVE-POTENCY).

- Obligation (1) is split into three premises:
  - reception only in the thing's own essence: unattributed, because De ente
    c.4 [69875] and q.7 a.2 state it only for created intellectual substances
    and subsistent created forms;
  - receiver in receptive potency to the received esse: De ente c.3
    [69874], sourced;
  - nothing in receptive potency to itself: unattributed.
- Obligation (2) is the q.7 a.1 warrant restricted to esse: limited esse is
  received.
- Obligation (3) is that unlimited esse suffices for the old primitive
  `Infinite`.

The exact legacy `SubsistentBeingIsInfiniteReading` is derived without the
legacy not-both law or `Composite`, so the actualized-receptive-potency
boundary above is kept. SUBSISTENT-INFINITY-RECEPTION-CONTROLS shows that each
premise is needed. The two unattributed generalizations, and the question
whether reception exhausts limitation, remain the open burdens
([ATTEMPT-SUBSISTENT-INFINITY-FROM-RECEPTION-20261105](../../corpus/attempts/subsistent-infinity-from-reception-20261105.json)).

## Recipient-relative limitation and source-scoped ascent (2026-11-17)

**Current conclusion.** The existence-only source-first route can use a weaker,
better delimited ascent principle: an actual esse–essence identity bearer
strictly exceeds the actual others whose esse it causes. The new derivation
establishes absolute nonlimitation of that source through the retained reception
premises before comparing it with a limited recipient. It does not treat freedom
from **this** recipient's constraint as freedom from every constraint. The
remaining absolute-limitation/degree comparison is substantive and explicit.
The original all-causes ascent and recipient-relative principles remain direct
support options; neither is established or refuted by the new derivation.

**Target and reuse.** The load-bearing input is `cffad050`, used by argument
`f4df2153` to turn common esse ancestry and composition into fixed-esse maximal
causality `da09703b`. Its received-limitation derivation `fa1d11a9` / result
`d9958e2c` uses `7030af77` (`UnlimitedPossessionExceedsLimited`). The latter's
existing degree derivation is `Round2PerfectionPrimacy.unlimitedPossessionExceedsLimited`,
result `8c6d4065`. Related/dossier/brief/novelty/context retrieval and the dossiers
of all eight new support inputs were inspected. Existing batch/rejected-edge,
reception-limitation, transfer and possession-only work is reused, especially
[the modal-spine capacity/order assessment](modal-spine.md) and
[the same-witness existence route](maximum-definition-fork.md#current-existence-bundle-judgment-select-the-cause-before-identifying-the-maximum-2026-11-17).
The warning about relative versus absolute nonlimitation is retained evidence,
not a new discovery. The scope reduction and the two linked consequence tests
below are this continuation's new formal work. No historical witness was fetched
or newly attributed.

**What the old step says.** With the existing dictionaries fixed, `7030af77` is
`InAct c r → LimitedBy x r x → ¬ LimitedBy c r x → MoreIn r c x`.
No cause hypothesis occurs in it. In `d9958e2c`, actual cause-of-esse supplies
source actuality; the effect's caused perfection is received and limited in it;
nonreception of the cause's perfection in that effect plus limitation-implies-
reception excludes that one limit. The final comparative step is entirely
`7030af77`. The earlier degree proof makes the extra assumption explicit:
`UnlimitedHavingExceedsCapacity` says any actual possessor not limited by z
exceeds z's capacity. Together with the effect's degree being within its own
capacity and greater-degree-implies-MoreIn, this yields strict superiority.
It does not derive that extra assumption from relative causal independence.

A capacity bound and an actual receiving role must be distinguished. If
LimitedBy means a numerical bound, exceeding the bound can support the degree
inference when comparison is applicable; merely falling within another's
capacity does not establish actual reception there. If it means the restriction
actually imposed by a recipient, absence of that role need not remove a limit
imposed elsewhere. The existing bridges connect these contents; their names do
not prove that connection. This is a comparison of application accounts for the
old premises, not a newly completed sense fork or a redefinition of LimitedBy.

**A discriminating consequence, not an imagined counterexample.** New result
`1de3bff1` / argument `534c19d4` has two checked conjuncts:

- `7030af77`, limitation-implies-reception `39a4c28c`, asymmetric MoreIn and
  explicit excluded middle force `ReceivedIn a r b ∨ ReceivedIn b r a` for any
  two actual self-limited possessors. Without either reception, the relative
  step makes each strictly exceed the other, contradicting asymmetry.
- Under the explicit esse recipient dictionary
  `ReceivedIn a () b := Received a (p.essenceOf b)`, the same consequence plus
  own-essence reception forces `p.essenceOf a = p.essenceOf b` for every two
  actual self-limited possessors. The one-point respect represents esse only;
  no nonesse interpretation is imposed.

This identifies an internal application problem for a blanket role-based
reading. The positive reception account itself allows finite received natures
of different kinds, with each act received in its own essence. That supplies
a mechanism and justification for examining non-cross-receiving recipients:
reception belongs to their distinct own quiddities, rather than to one another.
The certificate exposes what the relative bundle would require of that account;
it is not a certificate that a metaphysically possible pair or an actual esse
cause of equal degree has been found. Its essence equality is conditional on
the displayed recipient-role interpretation and on both self-limitation facts.
If that interpretation is declined, its actual application must be replaced,
not silently inferred from the word capacity. The old omission controls are
retained as dependency evidence only; no new arbitrary finite fixture is added.

Thus the strongest supported rival mechanism is finite received actualities
with distinct determining recipients, and law/capacity-mediated efficacy that
need not itself be a higher formal instance of the produced perfection. The
source tradition's received finite natures and the earlier capacity/order
analysis support this mechanism against the **role-to-degree inference**.
They do not establish an equal-grade contemporary productive cause of esse.
No substantive counterexample to the narrower unreceived-source ascent was
identified. Originating human generation cannot refute it merely by having an
equal-kind offspring; its inclusion in the selected esse-cause dictionary would
itself need warrant. The current productive-support application remains separate.

**New strength sibling and positive support bundle.** Statement `8176a284`,
`SourceScopedAscent.IdentityCauseAscent`, transparently restricts `cffad050` to
actual source and target, with `p.esseOf c = p.essenceOf c`. It introduces no
new causal or degree relation. Result `30c1203b` / argument `32127cb9` derives
it from `cffad050` alone; relation `c767746e` registers that strength order.
This is a scope restriction, not a sense change. No strictness countermodel or
absolute logical minimality claim is made. Direct primitive acceptance remains
available alongside the following derived support option.

Result `62bd2829` / argument `142d2d1d` uses exactly eight existing inputs:

- own-essence reception `6dfe01ab`, receiver-to-act potency `7fcfcee8`, and
  no self-receptive-potency `14325c83`;
- caused esse is received `da72ae93`, received esse is limited `7de89edb`, and
  limited esse requires some reception `1304b462`;
- absolute limited-esse-approaches-less `fd2c8710`, and greater-degree-is-MoreIn
  `9eaa3b6e`, the latter instantiated at the one-point esse respect.

Any alleged reception of the identity source would put its esse in receptive
potency to itself. Excluding **all** such reception and applying `1304b462`
therefore excludes absolute limitation of that source. The distinct effect is
received and limited through the explicit causal receipt and limitation inputs.
Only then do the last two interfaces yield strict MoreIn at the SAME esse.
No generic perfection-to-reception, no anti-reception-in-this-effect premise,
no relative-capacity-exceeds premise and no transfer-to-every-perfection is
used. This does not erase the new bundle's application obligations. In
particular, deriving `da72ae93` through its existing Fourth-Way route would
return through the target; here its direct receipt rationale is the support
option. The broad imported module's theological conclusions are not premises.

The positive warrant is participation and derivative efficacy: the recipient
has a delimited act through another, while the unreceived source's act is not
contracted by a receiving principle. If MoreIn measures formal fullness of
esse, strict limitation versus unrestricted fullness motivates a strict
comparison. This is more informative than nonlimitation by one individual.
`fd2c8710` and the degree calibration remain the explicit generalization of that
account, not a definition of the desired order. Degrees range over a supplied
preorder, not physical quantities; total comparison or a numerical infinity
is not assumed. Defining a binary degree from the Limited predicate would make
one interface automatic but transfer the entire warrant burden to degree-to-
MoreIn. That construction would not be corroboration and is not added.

**Strictness, possession and application.** Mere noninferiority does not identify
a maximal m with the source n: a tied m can still have no strict superior.
The new proof needs strict MoreIn only from the actual identity source to a
distinct actual m, not from every received intermediate cause. Inclusive
formal-or-eminent InAct is retained. Power to produce a perfection, formal
exemplification of it, eminent containment and a higher common degree are
separate claims. Neither a formal temperature comparison nor merely naming a
power eminent warrants the esse comparison. If eminence is understood as power,
that can motivate causal possession while leaving strict degree superiority
substantive. If it means independently specified containment, its application
requires that content. No new eminence sense is silently installed here.

**Exact consumer and cumulative judgment.** Result `47efe91a` / argument
`0609389b` replaces only `cffad050` by `8176a284` in the accepted four-input
source-first argument; common source `c35b1a5c`, composition `b4987085` and
excluded middle `ee4ab504` are retained. The source's previously unused identity
is now consumed. Composition gives its esse-causal scope, and scoped ascent
identifies every actual maximum with that same n. The unchanged fixed-esse
consumer `c98328f1` / result `d8cb586a` carries `da09703b` into GodExists with
its other eleven inputs. There is no new God naming or witness substitution.
Common-nature/source selection, ancestry/no-regress, composition, existence,
actual-ground correspondence, transfer and nominal-description burdens remain.
A fundamental natural universal source is not excluded by this stage's name;
later attributes and identification remain distinct.

Provisional judgment: prefer the **source-scoped absolute-limitation bundle**
as the available positive rationale for this existence route. It avoids a
specific overreach of the blanket recipient-relative account while preserving
participation's substantive explanatory content. The old relative rule may be
considered directly under an independently defended capacity/order application;
the new results do not settle that application or disprove full causal ascent.
The stricter-source conclusion is conditionally supported, not established;
no new theorem establishes receipt, limitation, grading or real-world productive
support. The rival above targets an actual inference rather than merely
asserting another limiter is conceivable, and its relevance stops short of a
counterexample to the absolute unreceived-source account.

**Rejected approaches and next test.** Rejected: paraphrasing relative freedom
as absolute unlimitedness; treating the degree decomposition as independent
warrant for its beyond-capacity input; importing physical degree or formal
possession into an eminent-esse comparison; substituting no-less for strict
more; redoing generic omission fixtures; and returning through Fourth-Way
causation to justify its receipt input. The scoped consequence and its common-
source application are resolved under the displayed inputs. A distinct next
warrant test is `fd2c8710` with `9eaa3b6e`: specify the same esse-fullness degree
independently of the desired source/effect order, and explain what makes a
limited recipient strictly below the unreceived source on that degree while
preserving formal/eminent possession. Reopen the broader relative route with
a justified capacity calibration that answers the cross-reception/receiving-
essence consequence, or a positively warranted same-esse counterexample. More
models or a renamed order axiom would not answer these questions.

**Claim/type self-sweep.** New ascent vocabulary is a transparent restriction,
not an opaque metaphysical flag. Old definitions, claims, Challenges and source
attributions remain unchanged; no new source claim or established status is
created. The audit has explicit internal hypotheses and asserts no actual pair
or premise refutation. All proof inputs use the same respective cause, receiver,
limitation, comparative and esse dictionaries. Principle-valued reception is
not silently equated with recipient-being reception: their adapter appears in
the second audit conjunct only. The source support proof uses the former
throughout. Its world-free, mode-unspecified CIn does not become present
productive support through this proof. Final focused verification, computed
frontier comparison and reproduction evidence follow below; fresh independent
semantic review of this full proposed outcome precedes publication.

### Draft continuation: esse comparative ascent (2026-11-24)

## Independent fullness candidates and rival comparison (2026-11-17)

**Proposed judgment.** The positive participation/whole-power rationale remains
a reason to consider strict source fullness directly. Two independently defined
comparisons now distinguish what its current support earns: complete inclusive
perfection coverage gives noninferiority, with strictness exactly when the
recipient misses a pure perfection; the accepted common source has strictly
larger **actual esse-effect coverage** than every other actual bearer. Neither
result identifies its set order with MoreIn esse. The esse-causal result applies
equally to a fundamental natural source satisfying the same restrictions.
It therefore supplies positive causal information without deciding divine identity.

**Exact target, reuse and route.** The inputs are
`fd2c8710-988f-527f-a81a-2566fe10e3f9` (limited esse approaches less) and
`9eaa3b6e-ad2f-5776-ab26-03cfa9f54ad8` (greater degree is MoreIn), used by
reception argument `142d2d1d` / result `62bd2829` to support actual identity-source
ascent `8176a284`. The retained route then runs through common source and
composition in `0609389b` to fixed-esse causality `da09703b`, and through the
unchanged twelve-input `c98328f1` / `d8cb586a` to GodExists. Required related,
dossier, brief, novelty and context retrieval, including both target dossiers,
preceded these tests. Reuse includes `Round3FourthWay.AsMuchAsItApproaches`,
`MoreInMatchesDegreeEverywhere`, the degree-fork audit, the earlier capacity/
eminence assessment in [modal-spine.md](modal-spine.md), and the previous
source-scoped ascent findings above. No duplicate q11 inquiry is opened.

Coordinator repairs `9fda12bf6` and `1ae1ba3b3` are incorporated locally as
`312e0669e` and `1ae25ddb3`. The retained own-essence interface explanation and
the two repaired owning-spec paraphrases are preserved. They change no proof.
No further old statement prose is changed in this continuation.

**Strongest positive warrant.** The retained whole-power argument `d164b1ff`
(SCG I.28 reconstruction), with pure-perfections-pertain-to-being `c4fb87a6`,
gives a substantive account: a received nature determines a contracted way of
being, whereas what is its own subsistent act does not receive that act within
a further determining nature. If every positive actuality perfects being,
unreceived esse lacks no excellence of being. This explains why the source's
fullness is supposed to exceed a contracted recipient, rather than merely
postulating a larger number for the source. The argument can be accepted on
its prima facie explanatory merits; primitives are not worthless without an
endlessly prior proof. These are retained source reconstructions and warrant
reasons, not fresh source readings or proof that their actuality dictionaries
apply. Represented identity, unreceivedness, OwnBeing and whole-power possession
remain distinct interfaces. In particular the real-determination Has of the
whole-power theorem is not silently identified with inclusive eminent possession.

Q3's approach order is not a homogeneous physical measure; its source reading
orders likeness to a primary rather than assigning physical units of being.
The existing `AsMuchAsItApproaches` and comparative calibration connect supplied
orders; they do not independently specify what likeness or fullness consists in.
Invoking them in both directions would not corroborate fd2c8710. A proposed
value must get its content without defining it as "higher precisely when the
recipient is limited and the source is not".

**Test 1: independently supplied perfection coverage.** Result `e3167aef` /
argument `89af2457` uses the existing inclusive ActFrame and independently
supplied Pure classification. `possessionContent F Pure x` is exactly the set
of pure perfections formally or virtually possessed by x. It consults neither
Limited, MoreIn, Godhood nor source/recipient labels. For c with every pure
perfection in this SAME sense, the checked result proves:

- x's content is included in c's;
- strict inclusion iff some pure perfection is absent from x **even inclusively**;
- equality iff x also has every pure perfection inclusively.

The completeness hypothesis is explicit, not inferred from the name source.
This is positive noninferiority support when a faithful whole-power/possession
application supplies it. Strictness has an exact further warrant burden. A
binary repertoire can omit intensity or ways of possession: having the same
perfection kinds need not record having them to the same degree. Conversely,
merely lacking a perfection formally does not show it absent virtually. No
actual complete limited recipient is asserted, and no counterexample to the
metaphysical fullness thesis follows from that conditional equality case.
Without source completeness, this ordinary partial order also admits
incomparable repertoires; mutual coverage failures identify incomparability,
not a tie. Neither situation is asserted of an actual unreceived source here.

The frame retains formal and virtual fields; the inclusive degree records that
one mode holds, without ranking the modes. Under power-only virtual possession
its content measures power-coverage, not independently specified substantive
containment. Under substantive eminence the interpretation owes what is
contained and why. The accepted formal/virtual distinction is preserved; no
new completed eminence sense or constitutive-content identity is claimed.
A repertoire containing intensity thresholds could be tested, but those
thresholds and their purity must be justified independently. Adding a
source-labelled perfection solely to obtain a missing witness would define
the desired result into the inventory.

**Test 2: actual esse-causal coverage of the same source.** Result `31055b56` /
argument `0db2354d` uses `actualEsseEffects p c := {x | ActualEsseCause p c x}`.
From the existing common ancestor `c35b1a5c` and composition `b4987085`, it
preserves one actual identity source n and proves strict set inclusion of every
other actual x's effects in n's effects. Chain collapse supplies n's causal
scope. An effect of x cannot be n, because n has no incoming actual cause;
hence it is also an effect of n. The actual x is an effect of n and cannot be
its own **distinct** effect, giving strictness. Unlike the preceding maximality
application, this test consumes the common source's no-incoming-cause clause.

This degree is independently fixed by the causal dictionary. It is actual CIn
coverage, not possible effects, capacity, productive intensity or
formal/eminent perfection. CIn remains mode-unspecified; a productive reading needs its own retained
application. It says nothing about activity in other worlds or times. It is therefore a specifically insufficient *basis for the
esse-grade conclusion* without calibration, despite being a genuine positive
causal consequence. No new bridge identifying this footprint with MoreIn is
registered. These two tests serve the named root inputs above; they are not
advertised as new root-reaching support arguments or premise discharges.

**Same explanandum and rival mechanisms.** Fix the same actual target bearers,
selected source n, esse respect and actual CIn relation. The shared conditional
task is the obtaining of those bearers' esse through that source, including
present support if the separately retained productive application is warranted.
Do not compare that task with mere origination or regularity of already
existing states. The source Profile itself remains tenseless and mode-unspecified.

The participation account explains recipients through restricted ways of
actuality deriving from unreceived act; its whole-power account additionally
motivates substantive perfective containment. The strongest natural-source
alternative is an actual fundamental reality with intrinsic active powers,
whose lawlike operation supplies the same recipients through intermediaries.
Laws describe its operative powers; an abstract law is not substituted for an
agent. Its candidate must meet the SAME unreceived/identity, selection, causal
coverage and composition conditions; calling it natural does not prove those
conditions, and calling it physical does not prove finitude or composition.
Those application debts also remain for the theistic candidate.

The positive basis for taking this alternative seriously is the retained
capacity/mechanism account of production and the explicit natural-ground
comparisons in [physical-ground-468.md](physical-ground-468.md#coverage-on-the-same-explananda).
They distinguish causal success from formal exemplification and from candidate-
selected purity criteria. Their tables are interpretations, not actual or
metaphysical possibility certificates. They do not by themselves establish a
universal present natural source. The retained immanent-infinite-source reading
in that note offers a further option accepting source fullness rather than
denouncing it. Its translation-based historical evidence is reused, not freshly
read or newly mapped into the current substantial-bearer/CIn dictionary.
Its separate contingency/agency commitments are not settled by this degree test.

**Comparative verdicts, with their scope.**

| Account or inference | Assessment from the present evidence |
| --- | --- |
| Participation plus substantive whole-power possession | Positively motivated; can be retained as a direct primitive/cumulative option. These audits do not establish strict fd2c8710 or its application. |
| Perfection-kind coverage alone as the strict esse degree | Specifically insufficient without the missing-perfection or independently justified intensity witness. This is a measurement limitation, not an actual defeat of participation. |
| Fundamental natural source meeting the retained source conditions | Still a live explanatory proposal under these same restrictions and earns the same conditional strict footprint; a full same-background model and actual adequacy are not certified. Power-only eminence is insufficient for substantive containment, not thereby false as a causal account of the SAME recipients. |
| An immanent unlimited source accepting perfective fullness | Not excluded by this step. Strict fullness alone does not discriminate personal/divine naming or later attributes. Its actual dictionary application remains open. |
| Formal models as evidence that either complete ontology is true or false | Specifically insufficient; no such inference is made. |

No complete rival is established false or internally undermined here. Nor does
the inspected evidence warrant ranking the entire natural account less supported
on the narrowly shared causal task merely because it declines a further
substantive-containment claim. A proposal that calls its source finite in the
SAME formal-perfection sense while retaining absolute nonlimitation would
contradict the retained restriction; naturalism as such entails no such proposal.
The participation account's extra explanatory content is worth investigating,
but absence of a derived grade interpretation is not a refutation of its
primitive warrant. No total or scalar order is required; the selected source/
recipient comparison itself must be warranted. Conversely, a source's productive success alone supplies no strict
perfective rank. These judgments do not infer compatibility or rival defeat
from the absence of a discovered objection.

**Cumulative judgment and retained case.** Retain restricted source ascent
`8176a284` as a conditional option with its positive participation rationale.
The two independent audits add useful distinctions and a positive actual-CIn
consequence; they are not independent corroboration of fd2c8710 merely because
one can choose a calibration reproducing it. All common-source selection,
ancestry/no-regress, reception, composition, actuality, existence-to-ground,
maximum, transfer and naming inputs of the accepted case remain. Neither the
source's represented identity nor set inclusion names it God. No necessity,
intellect, will or Catholic identification is inferred, and q11 is not reopened.

**Resolved slice, rejected approaches and next test.** The two specified orders
are now tested. Rejected: reusing a binary unlimited/limited rank as independent
fullness warrant; causal depth as if it measured perfective actuality; actual
causal footprint as capacity or MoreIn; formal absence as inclusive absence;
and candidate-relative purity or inventory additions chosen to secure the

The remaining consequential test is the existing `d164b1ff` whole-power account
on the SAME selected n: does its real-determination/OwnBeing interpretation
warrant substantive eminent possession in the chosen act dictionary, and what

limited recipient? This is a concrete source of support for the still-open
fd2c8710/9eaa3b6e calibration, not another arbitrary measure or a duplicate
common-nature question. Reopen with that warranted mode/intensity account or a
supported same-background rival denying it; an actual intensity-sensitive
calibration would materially change this judgment. No independent actual
missing-perfection witness or such calibration was obtained in the present
candidate-order slice. Direct primitive warrant remains available without
pretending the conditional tests discharge it.

**Self-sweep.** Two completed set constructions introduce no new metaphysical

## Whole-power possession on the same source (2026-11-17)

**Proposed outcome.** The whole-power thesis is a positive account of subsistent
actuality, not a rule equating successful production with intrinsic possession.
Its exact encoding yields every **OfBeing** determination of an OwnBeing source;
it neither chooses the formal/eminent interpretation of Has nor encodes absence
of defects. New checked consequences make two commitments explicit: the full
whole-power/strict-purity bundle constrains OfBeing to coincide with Pure, and
the retained receipt/limitation/infinity readings supply a genuine OwnBeing
versus non-OwnBeing mode contrast for the same actual source and its effects.
A conditional infinite-versus-limited esse contrast is obtained under explicit
q7 readings. It provides prima facie perfective warrant within the act/reception
account, rather than merely a power difference. A particular mode-aligned pure-
perfection absence or independent calibration of the q3 approach degree is not
supplied; strict MoreIn across the required recipient domain is not certified. Direct
primitive and cumulative warrant remain available; this is not a refutation of
the participation account or a victory for a power-only source interpretation.

**Target, source and reuse.** The target is
`d164b1ff-ad31-5fbc-8ef9-eb0db51ebd96`,
`OwnBeingLacksNoPerfectionOfBeing`. Related/dossier/brief/novelty and parent
context retrieval were inspected, together with the exact OwnBeing,
real-determination, pure-perfection, inclusive act and recipient dictionaries.
The existing whole-power/root-perfection results, Q44 source supply, earlier
reception and scope controls, and the previous fullness audits are reused.
The accepted source claim `1c67e58f`, I.28 [2–3], rests on the previously read
Pegis translation; it is retained evidence, not a fresh source reading or a
new Latin attribution. The source's whole-power reasoning proceeds from
excellence according to being and own subsistence, with unreceived whiteness
as analogy. It does not proceed from an agent's bare power to cause each effect.
The actual-source application and the generalization from that analogy remain
metaphysical warrant questions.

The coordinator's label repair `21b696ec1` is carried locally as `884c95404`,
only for inference `bfc46c9a`; coordinator-owned imports and status generation
are untouched. Earlier interface and owning-spec repairs remain intact.

**Same-witness application before possession.** Fix the n supplied by common
source `c35b1a5c` on the SAME Profile, actual target domain and esse respect.
The existing `Q44SourceSupply.ownBeingOfIdentity` applies to its represented
identity only under own-essence reception `6dfe01ab`, receiver-to-act potency
`7fcfcee8`, irreflexivity `14325c83`, and the explicit unreceived-to-OwnBeing
reading `bc1810e5`. Thus it gives OwnBeing n without defining OwnBeing as map
identity. The broader own-essence interface remains interface reuse, with its
accepted `strong_version_needed` explanation and the unbuilt faithful adapter
to identity-scoped `b9316115`; no extra metaphysical scope is claimed needed.
Composition `b4987085` supplies n's direct esse scope through the retained
ancestry. Whole-power then gives Has n d from OfBeing d; `c4fb87a6` gives that
for every independently classified Pure d. No source switch, God predicate or
mere source-of-power description performs any of these steps.

**Positive warrant and its scope.** The strongest rationale is that a received
act is had under a determining nature, while subsistent esse is not an act
contracted within another receiver. Since positive actualities perfect being,
own subsistent act is proposed as the unrestricted ground of their excellences.
This is substantive prima facie warrant for d164b1ff, independently of a
particular causal-ability table. One need not demand a prior proof of every
primitive before considering it. What is not thereby supplied is a faithful
inventory of real determinations, its modal interpretation in each bearer, or
an intensive order measuring them. The source gloss's perfection and no-defect
claims must not be used to expand the primitive OwnBeing predicate so that the
whole-power argument becomes an unfolding of a stronger definition.

**Checked scope cost and the no-defect clause.** Result `77882a3c` / argument
`02d16349` has two conjuncts. Under d164b1ff, pure-perfections-pertain-to-being,
own-being-is-no-recipient `7338142d` and nonrecipient-determinations-are-pure
`32695660`, one OwnBeing witness n entails **OfBeing d iff Pure d**, for every d.
The forward direction is OfBeing → Has n d → Pure; the converse is the retained
pertain-to-being premise. This constrains the determination vocabulary; it does
not say that every being is pure or that finite features cannot exist.

Consequently an independently warranted mixed determination (OfBeing d and
not Pure d) cannot coexist with all four interfaces and an OwnBeing witness.
This is a conditional conflict, not evidence that such a determination has
been found in the intended vocabulary or that the whole metaphysics is false.
It identifies the cost of combining broad OfBeing, literal real possession and
strict source purity. A principled pure-positive-core reading, a justified
formal/eminent account of possession, or revision of the extra purity
application can avoid that combination. None is automatically licensed merely
because it saves a preferred candidate. In particular the source's own allowance
for opposed creaturely forms to preexist eminently bars reading its conclusion
as possession of every incompatible finite form in the same formal way.

The second conjunct proves **extension stability**: enlarging Has preserves the
positive whole-power clause. Thus d164b1ff alone does not prohibit adding another
determination; the omitted no-defect clause is not encoded by positive
completeness. The extra purity premises separately constrain such additions.
Nor is “not Pure” simply “a defect”: the existing classifier includes receiving
and individuating principles and potencies. Receptive potency may be actualized
and is not the absence of the act received. Strict purity, no defect, and
perfection within a finite nature are different claims. This audit introduces
no Defect flag and assumes none of those equivalences.

**A checked actual-mode difference.** Result `94271978` / argument `32ac7e4e`
starts with that same supplied actual n, OwnBeing n and existing universal esse
scope. It retains caused-esse reception `da72ae93`, received-esse limitation
`7de89edb`, OwnBeing-to-infinity `10b2b0ed`, and infinity-to-unlimited-esse
`965d0e26`. Every other actual x has received limited esse. OwnBeing x would
make it infinite and hence unlimited, contradicting that limitation. The result
preserves actual n, OwnBeing n, Infinite n and not Limited n; for each other
actual bearer it concludes Limited x, not Infinite x and not OwnBeing x. These
are the existing perfection-of-esse classifications, not physical sizes. No d164b1ff, degree, no-defect clause or intellect input
is used. The scope of the causal receipt premise, including the existing
individuation/application condition, is unchanged.

This is a real subsistence/infinity/limitation contrast under the stated
readings, not merely an actual-effect count or productive capacity. Where the
q7 classifications faithfully express unlimited versus contracted perfective
actuality, it positively motivates strict comparison as a primitive bridge;
no total or quantitative scale is required. It does not itself fix the q3
approach values or MoreIn, and is not counted as independent corroboration
merely because those values can be chosen to match it. It is also not yet a real-
determination or pure-perfection witness: OwnBeing is a bearer predicate, not
a supplied element of the determination/perfection carrier. Treating a generic
subsistence mode as a positive perfection is a possible primitive judgment;
its perfective classification and intensity must be defended rather than
manufactured by inserting “is this source” into the inventory. The proof does
not claim a new argument for common nature or independently warrant source
selection. It also does not infer not-OwnBeing from receipt by reversing
`bc1810e5`; the additional infinity/limitation readings do the work.

**Exact bridge still owed at the root.** In a faithful common determination/
respect dictionary, the source needs Has n d → inclusive InAct n d for the
relevant Pure d. A recipient's lack of real Has is not enough to infer inclusive
absence: that step needs inclusive InAct x d → Has x d at the selected target.
These are opposite directions, not a harmless relabelling of one predicate.
If the carriers differ, a meaning-preserving interpretation is required before
either direction is even an application. Real Has includes intrinsic
constituents or real relations, not every true extrinsic denomination; an actual
CIn footprint does not automatically become such an intrinsic determination.
Conversely, merely having a power to produce a perfection does not establish
substantive containment of that perfection. Formal absence also does not exclude
virtual possession. No global biconditional stronger than these local needs is
silently demanded, and no such adapter is asserted established here.

Once source completeness is faithfully transported, the accepted content audit
`e3167aef` gives coverage; strict coverage still needs a particular Pure d absent
**inclusively** from the actual recipient. Calibrating that coverage or an
independently specified intensity as an esse degree remains `fd2c8710` /
`9eaa3b6e`. It would then feed source ascent `8176a284`, fixed-esse causality
`da09703b`, and the unchanged GodExists consumer `c98328f1`. These are the named
bridge and blockers, not new root support edges from the present audits.

**Perfection candidates tested, with different verdicts.**

| Candidate | Warranted consequence and present verdict |
| --- | --- |
| The recipient lacks esse itself | Incompatible with the SAME actual-target premise InAct x esse. Rejected as a strictness witness; reception is not absence of the received act. |
| Subsistent rather than received act | The new mode result gives OwnBeing n and not OwnBeing x under explicit readings. A warranted contrast, with its pure-perfective interpretation and MoreIn calibration still open. |
| Infinite versus limited esse | The same-source q7 reading bundle gives the contrast on the full actual-recipient domain. Positive perfective warrant is available under that interpretation; the q3 approach/MoreIn calibration remains explicit. |
| Actual power to cause esse | Strict actual CIn coverage is already checked; an intermediate may also cause esse. Specifically insufficient as an intrinsic perfective or intensive comparison without the retained bridges. |
| A cognitive perfection | If independently Pure/OfBeing and possessed in the chosen substantive sense, d164b1ff commits the OwnBeing source to it. A source denying that possession must reject a named classification, mode bridge or whole-power input. No cognition from termination is inferred. A local noncognitive target would not cover the required arbitrary-maximum domain, and an intellectual recipient may share the perfection kind. |
| No defect or every determination pure | No-defect is not encoded by d164b1ff. The separately justified purity package has the checked classification cost; it is not obtained for free from positive completeness. |

An argument based on a known absent bodily form must also respect Pure scope:
the artisan/shape examples in the retained formal-versus-eminent audit explain
why modes cannot be identified, but do not refute a pure-scoped adapter unless
the relevant perfection's purity is independently justified. No arbitrary
source-indexed witness, omitted mode or model valuation repairs that burden.

**Natural and intellectual/divine proposals under identical restrictions.**
Both proposals must supply the same actual unreceived source, OwnBeing meaning,
causal scope, composition and reception application. Neither earns those facts
by its label. The active natural proposal explains the recipients through
intrinsic operative powers and lawlike activity; the intellectual/divine
proposal adds a substantive actual-perfection account of that activity.
Whole-power is a reason to investigate that added content, not a proof that
lawlike efficacy already is it. The retained immanent infinite-natural account
may grant positive fullness and thought; “natural” is not equivalent to a
finite, composite or intrinsically noncognitive source. The existing
[physical-ground comparison](physical-ground-468.md) supplies these rival
mechanisms and their boundaries, not an actual-world compatibility certificate.

There are consequential, symmetric commitments. If a natural proposal counts
an intrinsic limiting determination as real and non-pure, it conflicts with
the extra OwnBeing/no-recipient purity package; the identified conflict is not
naturalism's falsity. If an intellectual/divine proposal reads every finite
perfection as a formally replicated real determination, it faces the same
scope constraint. If it grants only the power to produce those perfections,
it has not established its claimed substantive containment. An intellectual
proposal that independently supplies a required cognitive actuality fits that
specific commitment better than one explicitly denying it, conditional on the
same pure classification and possession sense. That is local relative support,
not an overall divine-versus-natural verdict; an intellectual natural proposal
is not thereby excluded.

No complete rival is established false or epistemically self-undermining by
this slice. The incompatible candidate and packages above are identified by
their exact conditions. No assessment rests on unpopularity, bare possibility,
or a countermodel treated as an actual rival defeat. The mode contrast and
scope costs are useful positive and critical results even without worldview
refutation. The cumulative participation/whole-power rationale remains worth
retaining directly; a missing formal derivation is not evidence against its
prima facie merits.

**Stopping/reopening and reallocation.** The tested candidates yield a conditional subsistence and perfection-limit
contrast for every relevant actual recipient, but not a mode-aligned missing
pure perfection or an independently specified approach calibration. The precise remaining dependency is a substantive
mode/intensity interpretation: what real positive excellence is present in the
SAME OwnBeing source, absent or contracted in the recipient even allowing
eminence, and why that is MoreIn esse. The current virtual-act dictionary itself
records no concrete instance for its higher-way interpretation. This is not a
tooling failure or a requirement to prove every primitive from an earlier one;
the whole-power and comparative principles may remain explicit provisional
commitments. More transport lemmas or specially chosen inventories would expose
the same obligation. Reopen on an independently warranted instance or account
answering those mode, scope and intensity questions, with the complete rival's
same-background interpretation stated.

For a different consequential root test, the coordinator can next assess existing
nominal interface `1ec205ed` on the SAME selected source: what the fully described
antecedent warrants calling God, and whether an immanent/fundamental natural
source meeting it is included or excluded for substantive reasons. That would
use the retained fullness option provisionally, not discharge it or replace it
by a name. It is a recommendation, not a new naming argument or another q11
allocation. No historical/Catholic-specific bridge or other family's record
has been edited.

**Self-sweep.** Two closed audits use only existing dictionaries and explicitly
listed internal interfaces. They assert no source existence, new perfection
inventory, no-defect axiom, actual rival model or MoreIn calibration. The actual
source stays n; maps, recipients, real Has, inclusive possession and their
carriers are not identified by notation. Existing claims, source attributions,
old Challenges and established statuses remain unchanged. The source Profile
is still tenseless/mode-unspecified; present productive support and ordinary-
cause correspondence remain application burdens. The new mode proof is
independent of d164b1ff; the classification proof's added purity readings are
not misreported as part of its positive clause. Final focused verification,
computed comparison and exact reproduction evidence follow below. This complete
proposed judgment awaits fresh independent semantic challenge; no push, PR or
