## Current cluster assessment under the substantive objection standard (2026-11-18)

**Scope and boundary.** Issue #1751, slice 2, first drafted on main
`c49e6d2a65bd8881bcaaffdc6357535d20f21df1` and repaired after independent
semantic challenge on merged main `f85c68e20dc44f6259aaa65060c43a4a4f93fd9f`.
That merge added `abdc26b0` as a further consumer of `b26c10a0`. The cluster is the eighteen open
premises on GOD_4 routes whose attempts cite this note and which the
currentness view listed as `legacy_only`. Four closely governed neighbours are
added because this note fixes their meaning: unrestricted transfer `f8ba2429`,
universal-scope transfer `b26c10a0` and residual `5a4afaa5`, plus identity-source
ascent `8176a284`. None of the four is an open GOD_4 leaf. Earlier sections stay
unchanged under their own dates. This section restates their findings under the
2026-11-10 provisional-judgment, 2026-11-12 cumulative-warrant and
substantive-objection standards. It changes no theorem, Challenge, claim,
definition, closure or formal status. [Assessment record](../../corpus/attempts/5c7bc356-a1dc-4781-8548-24ad1ce06f79.json)

The source-selection detail for `11e24b50`, `cbb068e8`, `b4987085`, `6acd44eb`,
`e2bfd1b1` and `dcf4bcdd` lives in
[the q44 note](q44-inferential-dependency.md#support-comparison-and-provisional-warrant).
This section assesses those premises only as they serve this cluster. It does
not replace that note's judgments.

### (a) What the cluster supports, and under which fixed meanings

The meanings are the ones fixed in the earlier sections:

- `CausesIn c x r` is effective causal responsibility for x's actuality r; at esse
  it is responsibility for x's being. It is mode-unspecified and tenseless. It may
  run through secondary causes, and it is not exclusive, sufficient or temporally
  prior. `ActualEsseCause` adds actuality and distinctness on the same q3 profile.
- `EsseReceivedIn x r` (DEF-ESSE-RECEIVED-IN) means that x's esse is received in r
  as an act in a receiving potency. `ReceptivePotencyTo` is actualized receptive
  potency. It is not the legacy unactualized `InPotency`.
- `EsseLimited` is q.7's limitation of esse in formal perfection, not the
  three-disjunct `HasLimitingPrinciple` proxy. `OwnBeing` is the primitive
  subsistent-esse predicate of SCG I.28, not equality of the profile maps.
  `Infinite` is the primitive INFINITE_1, infinity of perfection.
- `MoreIn esse` is the primitive comparative. `9eaa3b6e` reads it by degree on a
  supplied preorder, with no physical quantity and no totality.
- `Has`, `OfBeing` and `Pure` are real determination, perfection of being and pure
  perfection. Participation is PARTICIPATION_4, q.44's derivative esse.

Under these meanings the cluster has four connected sub-bundles:

| Sub-bundle | Premises | What it conditionally supports |
| --- | --- | --- |
| A. Reception to own being | `7fcfcee8`, `14325c83`, `6dfe01ab` (used only at identity bearers), `bc1810e5` | Represented identity excludes reception and gives OwnBeing (`Q44SourceSupply.ownBeingOfIdentity`). It also gives infinity of identity bearers (`05bbe85e`, `3ed2829f`), identity-source ascent `62bd2829` (with `da72ae93`, `7de89edb`, `1304b462`) and at-most-one `07b1fac4` (with shared nature `75b5b027`). |
| B. Participation coverage and source supply | `11e24b50`, `cbb068e8`, `b4987085`, `6acd44eb`, `e2bfd1b1`, `dcf4bcdd` | Source supply `de05072a` and `8b8bb6df` (`9bbfe378`, `b171d107`), CREATURE_1 coverage `612078bf`, and, with `8176a284`, fixed-esse maximal causality `da09703b` (`47efe91a`) into the unchanged GodExists consumer `c98328f1`. |
| C. Fullness, infinity and degree | `d164b1ff`, `7338142d`, `32695660`, `10b2b0ed`, `fd2c8710`, `9eaa3b6e` | Strict MoreIn esse from the identity source (`62bd2829`), the source/recipient mode contrast `94271978`, the purity-cost audit `77882a3c`, strict primacy `c8002f3e` and the whole-power consumers `ff74a7ac`, `7d8bbdcb`, `95c04a6d`. |
| D. Causal transfer and the post-naming cause | `10cd24ed`, neighbours `b26c10a0`, `f8ba2429`, `5a4afaa5`; `86d02b69` | Unrestricted transfer through `a3d5fb1a` (with excluded middle and `b62a64ab`). Universal-scope transfer alone suffices for the naming, reduced-naming and goodness applications. `86d02b69` feeds GOD_4 maximality (`482f4ad2`, `4d1efe91`). |

The cluster supplies no source existence. That comes from the common-source and
ancestry inputs outside the cluster. It also supplies no present conservation
(tense bridge `19882fa9`), no numerical unity (`a576300f`, `75b5b027`), no GOD_4
naming (`1ec205ed`), no intellect and no necessity.

### (b) Strongest positive warrant for each central premise

"Direct option" means that the premise can be held on its own prima facie merits
alongside any derivation. Conditionality does not erase that option.

| Premise | Strongest positive warrant | Direct option and precondition status | Provisional standing |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `14325c83` no self-receptive potency | Receiving is a correlation between a potency and the act it receives, as in De ente c.3 [69874]. A principle that received itself as its own act would collapse the two roles that give the vocabulary its content. | A candidate conceptual primitive of the reception vocabulary. It is not a precondition of inquiry in general, and its general form is unattributed. | Strong prima facie in the actualized-potency sense. That identity excludes reception is the intended consequence, not a smuggled one. |
| `7fcfcee8` receiver in receptive potency | Stated claim `d9fde3af` (De ente c.3 [69874]), together with the act/potency account of reception. | Sourced. Applying it to an actual receiver requires the actualized-potency distinction, which is retained. | Strong as a source-internal reading. |
| `6dfe01ab` reception only in one's own essence | De ente c.4 [69875] and q.7 a.2 state it for created intellectual substances and subsistent created forms. | Generalization unattributed. Every inspected route application instantiates it only at identity bearers (`strong_version_needed` on `142d2d1d` and `07b1fac4`; the q44 supply lemma). Weaker siblings `b9316115` and `7590d341` are registered. | Broad form weak to moderate, under kept objection 3 below. The identity-scoped use is moderate and inherits `14325c83`'s strength. |
| `bc1810e5` unreceived esse is own being | q.7 a.1 co. and ad 3 assert both of God; the subsistent-whiteness illustration connects "not in another" with subsistence. | Supported analyst generalization. It is exactly as strong as the reception relation is complete, so it shares that dependency with `1304b462`. | Moderate. |

| `32695660` a nonrecipient's determinations are pure | q.7 a.1: form is limited through reception, and an undetermined form has the character of the perfect. | Supported generalization. The claim that individuating principles, potencies and privations occur only in recipients is the analyst's. | Weak to moderate. It carries the checked purity cost in row 6 below. |
| `10b2b0ed` own being is infinite | q.7 a.1 co. infers God's infinity from his being subsistent esse. | Supported. The represented-identity sibling `75a37a91` is derived through reception. No record identifies the two antecedents. | Moderate, and strong inside the q.7 account. |

Nearly analytic once MoreIn is read by degree on the same preorder. | Strong as a calibration. Its real load sits on which degree `fd2c8710` supplies. |
| `11e24b50` others participate | q.44 a.1 co. and ad 1 (supporting claim `11b0106d`): what is other than God has participated being. Identifying p with the essential-esse primary is the analyst's. | Supported. Depends on unique unreceived esse (`a576300f`, `75b5b027`). | Moderate, conditional on that uniqueness. |
| `cbb068e8` participant has distinct esse | Conceptual: to participate is to have being derivatively, so a participant is actual and is not its own act. | Analyst bridge with ordinary conceptual motivation. | Strong prima facie under PARTICIPATION_4. |

Shared dependencies stay visible:

  concurrence option, on the open source identification.
- `da72ae93` is shared by the ascent and the mode contrast.

`bc1810e5`, `7338142d`, `32695660`, `10b2b0ed`, `1304b462` and `7de89edb` are
presentations of one q.7 reception account. They are not independent
corroboration of one another.

### (c) Recorded objections and rivals, classified

| # | Record | Classification under the substantive standard |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | Secondary-only conservationist rival (`b0e72763`, background `66aab41c`; [shared-background tests](#shared-background-tests-and-surviving-rival)) | **Restated.** The earlier section's finding is retained unchanged: the rival "survives" in that the populated table jointly satisfies `TransferModelBackground` while falsifying `b26c10a0`. That is a non-entailment fact about one formal interpretation. The q.104 a.1 and q.105 a.5 distinctions (becoming against being, direct against indirect preservation, application to operation argued separately) show that concurrence is a thesis added to conservation. They do not independently justify that a universal source fails to apply secondary agents, so no objection is kept against `b26c10a0`'s direct option or against `2f6b9eff`/`791fa216`. The earlier requirement of an independent defense of active application stays open, and no rival weight is removed or added here. The Durandus label stays unwitnessed and is not adopted. Papineau and Hume are silent on this premise. |
| 2 | Scope of particular causes against `10cd24ed` | **No substantive objection identified**, matching `3de52780`'s own objection audit (2026-11-12). That attempt's rival, a particular cause of esse that does not cause a perfection another agent causes, is witnessed only in a model (control part 1). Its unweighted lead (a): if the q.104 a.1 heavenly bodies are nonuniversal causes of the being of what they generate, `10cd24ed` applies to them non-vacuously and requires that they cause every perfection of those effects, intellectual ones included. The lead is pending the `c3c01e92` bridge and a reading of q.115 a.4. Its lead (b), the supply example, is enablement, not `CausesIn … esse`, and carries no weight. A stronger particular-cause mechanism was proposed in this section's first draft (`86bcd73`) and withdrawn on review: nonuniversality excludes causing the esse of some *being*, not causing a perfection of an effect whose esse the cause does cause. If only a universal cause causes esse, `10cd24ed` is vacuously true. |

The two strength projections `46ce053d` and `564f13a8` derive the weaker siblings from it. Exact negation `4b23fd7c` and refutation `8f277485` are **restated** as conditional contradictions under the open premises `aab8ebd6` and `60b32382`, not as an unconditional defeat. |
| 4 | Fundamental natural source (`physical-ground-468.md`; "Independent fullness candidates" above) | **Restated** as an explanatory comparison at matching scope, not an objection to any premise of this cluster. It accepts the same unreceived-source, selection, coverage and composition conditions and earns the same conditional footprint. The earlier verdict table records that it may decline substantive perfective containment; whether it accepts `d164b1ff` or the OwnBeing reading is left open. It denies neither `fd2c8710` nor `b4987085`. Its relevance is the naming stage (`1ec205ed`) and the later attributes. |

It would become an objection to `32695660`'s extension only with an independently warranted mixed determination of being. None is identified. |

The relative bundle forces cross-reception between limited possessors, while the reception account allows distinct finite natures, each receiving in its own essence. Whether the law holds generally or only for per se series is a meaning question, not a failure mechanism. |

Stated and supported claims are positive warrant evidence. Leaf counts and route
costs are diagnostics only. No evidence was deleted.

Tally:

  premise.
- **restated** — 8 records (rows 1, 4, 5, 6, 8, 9 and 11, plus row 3's

- **no substantive objection identified** — rows 2, 5, 8 and 10, row 1's

### (d) Overall provisional judgment, revision conditions and next test

**Judgment.** The meanings are fixed in (a). On those meanings the source-scoped
core of the cluster forms a coherent, positively motivated participation
account: represented identity, then unreceived esse, then OwnBeing, then
nonlimitation and infinity, together with participation coverage, actual
esse-cause composition and identity-source ascent. It has moderate prima facie
warrant and conditionally reaches fixed-esse maximal causality. It does not
settle its own primitives, the q3 degree calibration, numerical unity or GOD_4
naming.

The transfer wing is weaker and asymmetric.

- Universal-scope transfer `b26c10a0` is conditional on three open, unassessed
  analyst premises (`5e281daf`, `2f6b9eff`, `791fa216`), plus a prima facie
  participation and concurrence option. That option needs the open
  source-identification step before naming, on pain of circularity. Its standing
  is unchanged from the earlier record.
- Nonuniversal transfer `10cd24ed` has no positive warrant on file and no
  substantive objection identified. It is an open bare premise, vacuous if only a
  universal cause causes esse. The q.104 a.1 heavenly-body lead bears against that
  vacuity.

`b26c10a0` has a non-transcription derivation and a prima facie direct option,
while `10cd24ed` has no derivation and no positive warrant for its direct option. Routes should therefore consume `b26c10a0` wherever
universal scope suffices, which is already true of naming, reduced naming and
goodness.

The fundamental natural source stays a live alternative at this stage, under
identical restrictions. Nothing here establishes a premise or defeats a rival.

**Revision conditions.** Each of the following would revise this judgment:

- An independently warranted mixed determination of being. This would revise
  `32695660` and the purity package.
- A positively warranted subsisting-and-received esse within the identity-bearer
  scope. This would reach the route's use of `6dfe01ab`.
- An independently justified account of conservation without concurrence at the
  scope of a universal source. This would be an objection to `b26c10a0`.
- An assessment of `5e281daf`, `2f6b9eff` and `791fa216`, or a non-circular
  pre-naming identification of the universal esse source with the subsistent first
  agent. Either would change `b26c10a0`'s conditional standing.
- Reading q.45 a.5, or deciding `c3c01e92` for q.104's heavenly bodies. Either
  would fix whether `10cd24ed` is vacuous or carries real content that needs positive warrant
  (q.115 a.4 is a further lead). Positive evidence that a nonuniversal esse cause fails to
  cause some perfection of its own effect would be a substantive objection.
- An independently specified degree of esse-fullness that does or does not place
  a limited recipient strictly below the source. This would move `fd2c8710` and
  `9eaa3b6e`.
- A defeat of `a576300f` or `75b5b027`. This would revise `11e24b50` and selection.

**Next discriminating test.** Run the consumer audit that `3de52780` deferred.
For each of the 43 non-cycle consumers of `f8ba2429` (45 at baseline, less the
cycle members `0bb5f653` and `992770d3`), decide whether it needs
nonuniversal scope or can consume `b26c10a0`. Then materialize q.45 a.5 and
decide `c3c01e92`, so that `10cd24ed` is either vacuous or a contentful premise
whose warrant must then be sought (q.115 a.4).

This is horizontal reallocation. The `fd2c8710` calibration has met the same
obstacle in three consecutive slices. Reopen it only with a substantive eminent
mode or intensity account on the same source, or with a warranted rival on the
same background.

**Formal residue, not new campaigns.**

- `dcf4bcdd` has only transcription support. The selected route avoids it via
  `8b8bb6df`.
- Consumers on the sense-index allowlist: `0a26eff0`, `38bef29c`, `46ce053d`,
  `564f13a8`, `7d8bbdcb`, `888b3cb6`, `95c04a6d`, `e6af6938` and `ff74a7ac`.
  These belong to the existing ratchet (#1233, #1524).
- The broad `6dfe01ab` is consumed for interface reuse by `142d2d1d`, `07b1fac4`
  and the q44 supply lemma. A faithful adapter to `b9316115`'s Frame would let

- The OwnBeing antecedent of `10b2b0ed` and the represented-identity antecedent
  of `75a37a91` are linked only in the identity-to-OwnBeing direction.

**Declared revisions: none.** No earlier influential judgment changes. The first
draft said that the conservationist rival's weight against `b26c10a0` was dropped
and that `b26c10a0` had moderate positive warrant. The independent challenge
identified that as an undeclared revision. Both claims are withdrawn rather than
declared:

- The earlier section's non-entailment finding, the `74008d2a` blocker requiring an
  independent defense of universal transfer, and the identification and
  non-circularity blocker are all retained unchanged.
- `b26c10a0` keeps its earlier conditional, unestablished standing.
- A trial `judgment` declaration on `b26c10a0` showed 13,625 potential consumers in
  `truth brief`, reached mostly through definition and statement references. That

- The remaining objection classifications restate boundaries that the earlier

  Its first provisional standing does not change the conditional validity of

- `truth brief` reported no pending revision consumers at baseline.

## GOD_4 maximal-cause route: universal-transfer sibling (2026-11-20)

**Scope and reuse.** Owner-selected follow-up to PR #1803 and epic #143.
Baseline main 65b79aed546ab0a1ec3b4905e484671179b494c7. The frozen argument 5d2b7c6e (result c160e427) has
eight jointly required inputs, including unrestricted cause-of-being-to-every-
perfection transfer f8ba2429. Its existing proof uses transfer only for the
MAXIME_1 witness obtained from 0881e859, which also causes the esse of every
other actual bearer. The already certified reducedDescription theorem in
TruthCore.Results.Aquinas.SummaTheologiae.CausalPerfectionTransfer.Solution
consumes precisely the weaker existing b26c10a0 universal-source transfer,
together with the same actuality-to-being, firstness, component-exclusion and
full GOD_4 naming premises. No new causal or transfer vocabulary is needed.

**Registered joint bundle.** New argument 0e8e37dc-4037-450f-8172-a26eff2d7113, result fd3330d0-dab7-4d44-9f51-790f196f8049,
proves the same existing conclusion 8626f8a5 from the original seven inputs
0881e859, 7067553d, cffad050, ee4ab504, 09c6d5f0, eaa4c679,
1ec205ed plus b26c10a0 instead of f8ba2429. Maximum uniqueness is retained
verbatim: if another maximum m' differs, m causes m' at esse and cffad050
makes m greater at esse, contrary to maximality. The same m is then passed
to reducedDescription; GOD_1 -> GOD_4 still depends on 1ec205ed. It is a
non-transcription conditional result. The original interface and its direct
assumption option remain unchanged.

**Strictness at fixed background.** Control statement e3471dfd-c005-4be4-a454-ab01a88e4b0d and
result fcc91ba5-c1ce-4a8e-b405-22d8912f9032 use exactly three bearers (source 0, supporter 1,
recipient 2) and respects (esse=false, further perfection=true). All have
esse, only the recipient has the further perfection; 0 causes every respect
of both others; 1 causes the recipient's
esse only. MoreIn esse is the strict ranking 0 > 1 > 2. PriorSimply and
ComponentOf are empty. IsGod holds exactly for 0. The six nonlogical inputs
shared with the original route (all but excluded middle ee4ab504), weakened transfer, and GodExists jointly hold, while
f8ba2429 fails at (supporter, recipient, further perfection). Classical
excluded middle is available separately. This finite interpretation is a
structural scope witness, not a non-theistic ontology or an objection to the
real-world truth of unrestricted transfer. It neither sets IsGod by
deduction nor supplies the source's existence independently of 0881e859.

**Warrant and boundaries.** b26c10a0 retains the direct participation/
concurrence rationale and the conditional total-causation derivation dae70d8a
(with open premises 5e281daf, 2f6b9eff and 791fa216). Pre-naming source
identification remains an explicit warrant challenge, not a discharged
bridge. Universal-source existence and coverage 0881e859, causal ascent
cffad050, maximal-to-first eaa4c679 and the nominal GOD_4 application
1ec205ed remain substantive. Direct use of 40a97664, C*, 2068f91c and
3ef67739 is absent in this bundle; recursively selected supports can still
involve the sustaining-dependence controversy. The existing analyses in this
note, maximum-definition-fork.md and esse-root-leaves.md retain their
current provisional assessments. The strictness test changes the conditional
sufficiency of the route, not their philosophical assessment.

**Status and next discriminating question.** This is a route-level scope
reduction, not evidence that b26c10a0 or the universal source is true. Review
the source-first warrant for universal esse-causal coverage and its
non-circular application before GOD_4 naming. Reopen the scope reduction if
the theorem proof fails at the same dictionary, or if a further route consumer
actually needs transfer by a nonuniversal cause. Do not count this sibling as
a second independent source of existential evidence. The formal result
status must be taken from exact-head verification, not this note.


## Formal effect, eminent cause and the cognitive first source (2026-11-22)

**Target and baseline.** This is a bounded follow-up to [#1731](https://github.com/moritzbrantner/formal-logos/issues/1731) on the already connected source `STMT-GOD-HAS-INTELLECT` (`29818edd`) and `STMT-GOD-HAS-WILL` (`66aecdd7`). The actual root and GOD_4 witness are supplied by existing routes, not created here. This work reuses `GradesFrame`'s shared `CAUSE_2` relation and IN_ACT_4, `TermSenseForks.ActFrame`'s formal/virtual/inclusive senses, q.4 a.2's `PerfectionsOfEffectPreexistInCauseReading`, and the already certified local-teleology/immateriality/same-root consumers. No new attribution to Descartes is made: `CartesianAdequacy` is an analyst navigation label, and the quoted Aquinas q.4 source reading retains its own witness and original uniform interface.

**Semantics of the precise inference.** The old q.4 predicate is
`∀ c x r, CausesIn c x esse → InAct x r → InAct c r`. Both occurrences of
`InAct` are the SAME primitive IN_ACT_4, although their natural interpretation
is asymmetric. The new sibling `FormalEffectPreexistsInclusively F A`
expresses `CausesIn c x esse ∧ A.InActFormally x r → (A.InActFormally c r ∨ A.InActVirtually c r)`.
The two directional calibrations are explicit:

1. `FormalActEntersGrades`: `A.InActFormally x r → F.InAct x r` at the EFFECT;
2. `GradesActIsInclusive`: `F.InAct c r → (A.InActFormally c r ∨ A.InActVirtually c r)` at the CAUSE.

`asymmetricFromUniform` composes exactly those two with legacy q.4. Neither
calibration defines IN_ACT_4, and neither follows from the other or from the
causal relation. A uniform formula with `InAct := formally` would exclude
the genuinely eminent cause; one with `InAct := inclusive` on both sides
admits *merely eminent effects*, so is not itself the asymmetric claim. The
source permits formally or eminently in the cause; the formal-effect restriction
is an analyst selection for a human intellectual capacity. Nothing here asserts
that every effect-perfection is formally possessed.

**New direct conditional connection, retaining its witness.**
`godContainsFromIntellectualExemplar` says that every already supplied GOD_4
bearer `g` contains a chosen intellectual perfection formally or eminently.
Its complete additional inputs, beyond the asymmetric q.4 result, are: GOD_4
`g` is an esse maximum; genus causality **at being only**
(`MaximumInBeingCausesAllBeingsReading`, the `r = esse` instance: every
maximum in being causes every other actual being's esse);
actuality implies actuality of esse; **one intellectual exemplar** `x` exists;
`HasIntellect x` is calibrated to *formal* possession of the chosen intellectual
respect; formal-to-IN_ACT_4 calibration; and excluded middle for the identity
of `x` and `g`. When `x = g` the formal possession directly settles the case.
When `x ≠ g`, the proof uses `CausesIn g x esse` and the very SAME `g`
as the conclusion, not a second maximum or an unaligned cause. The theorem
does not require an intellectual exemplar distinct from every GOD_4 bearer,
nor the stronger `UniversalCauseTransfersPerfections` that would claim
causal responsibility for each particular cognitive perfection. (Until the
challenge repair below, the theorem took the all-respect
`MaximumInGenusCausesAllInGenusReading`, which also asserts that any maximum in
the intellectual respect causes every other being's intellectual respect, a
particular-perfection causation commitment this route claims to avoid. The
proof used only the esse instance, so the hypothesis was weakened. The existing
GOD_4 routes that reach `hMaximal` still consume the all-respect genus premise,
which remains a shared dependency of the whole route, not of this step.) The existing q.4 first-mover theorem supplies a second
pointwise connection, `firstMoverContainsIntellect`, from one formally
intellectual exemplar and both calibrations; the mover relation is **exactly**
`F.MovesIn` from the supplied grades frame (only the `MovedIn` predicate is
external). The already registered first-mover/maximum identity remains needed
to identify that mover with a separately supplied GOD_4 or actual root; it must
use this very `F.MovesIn` relation, not an unrelated motion dictionary. These are code-level conditional
adapters, **not new registered argument edges**; the corpus's existing
GodHasIntellect and GodHasWill conclusions are not being redefined.

**What each conclusion means.**

| Claim | What the new work actually supplies |
| --- | --- |
| A first source causes a being with intellect | Conditional on the existing same-source/maximum, causal-genus and a formally intellectual exemplar. If that exemplar is the source itself, no new causal production is inferred; otherwise the maximum causes its **esse**, not necessarily its particular intellectual capacity or episodes |
| That source formally or eminently contains the cognitive perfection | Follows conditionally from the asymmetric q.4 principle, exemplar classification and explicit calibrations; the result uses the original first source, not an arbitrary intellectual producer |
| That source intrinsically **has intellect** (HAS_INTELLECT_1) | **OPEN.** `intrinsicIntellectOfContainment` explicitly requires separate formal-possession-to-intellect and eminent-possession-to-intellect recognition premises, each only at the GOD_4 bearers (so neither is asserted of secondary producers of cognitive beings); it proves only the modular logical handoff, not their warrant |
| That source is **actually understanding now** | **Not derived.** Even intrinsic capacity is not an occurrence; no exercise, intentional object, knowledge content or divine timeless actuality is inferred |
| That source **has will** (HAS_WILL_1) | Requires HAS_INTELLECT_1 plus existing `WillFollowsIntellectReading`; reuse `LocalTeleologyGodIntellect.godHasWill`, never identify eminent production, willing capacity and actual choice |

**Finite structural surrogates and rival.** `FormalEminentControl.Controls`
uses two beings (source `true`, cognitive effect `false`) and two respects
(esse `false`, intellect `true`). In its first table the effect is assigned
formal intellectual possession and the source virtual but **not** formal
possession; the source is the maximum and causes the effect's esse. Every
non-logical hypothesis of `godContainsFromIntellectualExemplar` holds jointly
(maximality, being-only genus, actuality-to-being, the exemplar and its formal
calibration, the effect-side calibration, asymmetric preexistence; excluded
middle is a logical principle), as do the cause-side calibration, uniform q.4
preexistence and the formal-recognition premise even in universal form, while
the GOD_4-restricted eminent-recognition premise fails: the primitive
`HasIntellect` stand-in holds only of the effect. In the second table the same
source causes the formally intellectual effect but is assigned neither formal
nor virtual cognitive possession: both calibrations, maximality and being-only
genus hold while asymmetric preexistence fails.

What this certifies, exactly: *in this primitive profile*, production with the
calibrations does not entail containment, and the listed bundle does not entail
the eminent-recognition premise. It is a **structural surrogate**:
`InActVirtually` and `HasIntellect` are independent primitives assigned by
stipulation. It is **not** evidence that eminent containment and intrinsic
intellect differ in meaning. Whether eminent containment in
DEF-IN-ACT-VIRTUALLY's sourced "has it in a higher way" sense includes
intrinsic intellect is **OPEN**; on that substantive-eminence reading the first
table is not an admissible model, and the table must not be used as evidence
on that reading. No capacity/exercise relation is formalized (an earlier
`allHaveCapacity`/`understandsNow` pair was unconnected to `HasIntellect` and
reduced to "a source exists"; it was removed in the challenge repair). The
controls are **not** a refutation of the metaphysical causal-adequacy premise
or a model of all God-existence premises.

The serious surviving positive rival interprets eminence as *productive
power*: an intellectually nonrepresenting first source sustains/grounds the
existence of beings whose cognitive capacities arise through determinate
noncognitive laws, mechanisms and secondary causal organization. It can grant
the positive q.4 formula under productive-power eminence; under a stronger
substantive eminent-presence reading it denies that its specified causes have
the required intellectual likeness. The route's strongest affirmative
rationale is that an adequate first agent explains more than the bare power
to yield cognitive effects: it supplies the actuality and intelligibility of
the form under which the effect can know. That claim must be articulated in
terms of intrinsic intentional apprehension, rather than equating mere causal
power to thinking by definition. Here the rival's production mechanism bears
directly on the missing recognition bridge, not on causal-genus coverage or
the existence of intellectual creatures.

**Cumulative judgment and change conditions.** The causal-perfection family
now has a precise *fourth candidate* path to inclusive cognitive containment,
not a fourth proved route to HAS_INTELLECT_1. The existing q.14 immateriality
route and local/global intellectual-governor routes already reach intrinsic
intellect with their distinct, unestablished bridge burdens, while this
perfection path adds a separate causal/effect argument that they do not
consume. Their common root/maximum, causal scope and God naming are not
additional independent evidence just because reused. **Provisional:**
formal-or-eminent containment at the specified causal first source receives
more articulated conditional support; intrinsic intellect and will gain a
new **conditional connection plan**, not a discharged premise or stronger
unqualified warrant. Both the direct recognition premise and its existing
support routes remain legitimate; the earlier assessments and the
particular-perfection causal-transfer rival are unchanged.

**Next discriminating test.** Specify a positive intentional operation
(representing an intellectual form and grasping intelligible content), then
compare formal and substantial-eminence readings against mere power-eminence
on the SAME instantiated source/effect/causal background. Test whether
substantial eminence supports *intrinsic* intellectual capacity without
presupposing HAS_INTELLECT_1, and retain a natural-power control with the
same productions. Only then is a registered connection to `29818edd`
justified; further actual understanding and capacity-level will need their
own unchanged application premises. Keep the source's still-open
philosophical recognition distinct from q.14's immateriality and local
teleology's first-source recognition; none is silently defeated.

**Delivery boundaries.** Existing statement IDs, source readings, direct
premise options, registered argument graph, and will modular consumer are
unchanged. The added Lean declarations are conditional helper theorems and
finite structural-surrogate certificates; the independent semantic challenge
and its repair are recorded below. Claim/type self-sweep: no new or retyped claim, source
attribution, corpus argument, result or selected-root consumer. Expected
`truth frontier-diff`: no premise change; the actual computed CI result is
the authority, not that expectation. Review of this added interpretation
must distinguish the theorem's exact antecedents, positive-model scope,
and intrinsic-recognition gap from a new full-root route.

### Independent semantic challenge repair (2026-11-22)

The independent semantic challenge of PR #1855 (reviewed head `ae52cc8e29`)
accepted the validity of all four theorems and the same-witness claim, and
raised two P1 findings, both repaired in the PR:

- **Surrogate overclaim.** The finite control had been described as showing
  that causation, inclusive possession and intrinsic intellect are
  "nonidentical meanings" and that the source is "eminently intellectual but
  intrinsically noncognitive". Because `InActVirtually` and `HasIntellect` are
  unconstrained primitives there, it shows only non-entailment in that profile.
  The note, attempt, `teleology-first-mover.md` and Lean doc comments now call
  it a structural surrogate (see "Finite structural surrogates" above).
- **Genus scope.** `godContainsFromIntellectualExemplar` now takes the
  being-only `MaximumInBeingCausesAllBeingsReading` instead of the all-respect
  genus reading. This weakens a hypothesis of an unregistered, unmerged
  Challenge theorem (the theorem becomes stronger); no registered result,
  argument or comparator configuration binds it.

P2 repairs: the two recognition premises of `intrinsicIntellectOfContainment`
are now restricted to GOD_4 bearers (the proof uses them only there; the
universal form would also give intellect to every noncognitive secondary
producer of cognitive beings and so begged the productive-power rival); the
degenerate capacity/exercise clauses were removed from the control, which now
also certifies actuality-to-being, the exemplar calibration and the
recognition pair, making the first table a joint nonvacuity witness of the
theorem's non-logical inputs; doc-comment wording was corrected.

**Recorded gap: the virtual/eminent sub-fork.** DEF-IN-ACT-VIRTUALLY's
sourced definition joins two readings: "a power to produce" the perfection
(productive-power eminence) and "has it in a higher way" (substantive
eminence). Lean has one primitive, `ActFrame.InActVirtually`, and no separate
declaration for either reading, so no sense record can yet be added with a
distinct Lean type. The fork is recorded here rather than in
DEF-IN-ACT-VIRTUALLY's `open_obligations`, because that record and
DISAMB-IN-ACT are hash-pinned by the reviewed classical-attributes bundle
audits (`scripts/bundle_audit.py`); adding it to the record needs that
audit's re-review. The warrant of the eminent-recognition premise differs
completely between the readings (weak on productive power, the live question
on substantive eminence); `intrinsicIntellectOfContainment` is neutral
between them. The fork must be split into distinct senses before any
registered connection to `29818edd` consumes the eminent-recognition premise.

### Coordinator follow-up on the second q.4 a.2 route (2026-11-22; UNREVIEWED)

The following findings come from a coordinator follow-up comment on PR #1855.
They have **not** had an independent semantic challenge. They are recorded as
provisional research leads, not as reviewed judgments, and nothing below
changes a recorded formal status.

- **Second q.4 a.2 route.** Besides efficient-cause preexistence, ST I q.4
  a.2 argues from subsistent esse that the first source has the whole
  perfection of being, including life and wisdom (ad 3). The corpus already
  represents this through `WholePowerPerfection.OwnBeingLacksNoPerfectionOfBeing`,
  `PurePerfectionsPertainToBeing` and
  `Results.Causality.WholePowerPerfection.rootPerfectionFromWholePower`, on each
  supplied actual root: `OwnBeing g`, `Pure wisdom` and the two universal
  inputs give `Has g wisdom`, bypassing any premise that the same cause
  produces each particular cognitive perfection. But `Has : B → D → Prop` is
  generic and separate from both `ActFrame.InActFormallyOrVirtually` and
  HAS_INTELLECT_1: a bare `d : D` named wisdom plus `Pure d` does not establish
  that `Has g d` is intrinsic truth-directed cognition. Extending this route to
  HAS_INTELLECT_1 needs an argument that the positive content of understanding
  is preserved in the source's analogical possession. A finite joint
  assignment (enumerated in Python, not Lean-certified) keeps causal
  preexistence and whole-being possession of `wisdom` while `HasIntellect`
  fails at the source; like the Lean control above, it is a structural
  surrogate and is not admissible if `Has g wisdom` by meaning constitutes
  intrinsic wisdom.
- **Caveat on result `c55e2ce2`.**
  `DeiFiliusOperationFromIntellect.intellectualPureActOperatesFromCapacity`
  derives a unary `Operates` from intrinsic intellect, pure act and three
  capacity/exercise premises, but the shared `InAct` in its exercise premise is
  inclusive formal-or-virtual. As interpreted, merely virtual cognitive
  possession could count as exercise. Neither `Operates g` nor inclusive
  `InAct g understanding` entails intentional representation or actual
  self-understanding. Do not clone that result or read it as an occurrence
  proof. For actual divine understanding, mode of possession
  (formal/eminent) and status of intentional operation (exercised or not,
  timeless exercise allowed) are two separate axes.
- **Matched-scope rival.** A single nonrepresenting first source sustains
  lawful secondary architectures with naturalized representation and rational
  capacities (reuse the producer/consumer, sensor/replay and thinking-source
  comparisons of `intentionality-943.md`, `form-selection-1380.md` and
  `mind-physicalism-fork-419.md`). It can grant productive eminence and must
  deny either that wisdom is a pure perfection of subsistent being or that
  analogical `Has` preserves wisdom's immanent truth-directed content.
- **Next test (A)/(B).** On the existing same-source GOD_4 / ActualRoot
  witnesses, hold causal sources and effects fixed and compare (A) generic
  virtual productive possession with (B) subsistent-being wisdom with
  intrinsic intentional content; state the weakest nontranscription bridge
  from `Has g wisdom` to source-borne cognitive understanding; separately test
  the pure-act capacity/exercise route of `c55e2ce2` against its virtual-acting
  reading. No GodHasIntellect argument should be registered until that bridge
  has semantic warrant and a checked result.
- **Unreviewed provisional ranking.** The comment judges the
  subsistent-being/whole-perfection route a "materially stronger positive
  support candidate for intrinsic wisdom" than bare causal production. This is
  an influential judgment that has not had its own challenge; it is recorded
  here and must not be relied on until reviewed.


## Intentional exemplars and possessed-good response (2026-11-23; stacked on #1866)

**Destination and scope.** The user-selected next question remains whether the
same necessary first source intrinsically understands, rather than merely
producing intellectual creatures. Merged #1855's formal/eminent containment and
draft #1866's intrinsic-self-knowledge result are reused unchanged. The new
Lean-only sibling `IntentionalExemplarExtension` examines what follows *if*
#1866's actual, content-indexed `IntrinsicSelfKnowledge(g)` obtains. It does not
establish any #1866 bridge, the GOD_4 witness, or a new registered graph edge.
The present draft requires #1866 first and its independent semantic challenge.

**A stronger productive-only test.** In the joint two-world/two-being Bool interpretation
`true` is the **necessary actual root** of the fixed profile at world `true`,
exists in both worlds, is pure act, and is a CAUSE_2/esse source of the
actual grounded secondary `false`. The profile shares a single
`CausalityFrame` with the same causal edge as the upstream #1866 control. The effect
`false` now genuinely `Apprehends(false,false)` and is
`CognitivelyGrounded(false,false)`, rather than only being labelled
`HasIntellect(false)` as in #1866. Its `reading(state,interference)` tracks
a changing target, responds to that reading, and exhibits both true and
erroneous reports when interference changes. The very same source possesses
the stipulated whole-being wisdom determination but apprehends no content.
This explicitly repairs the narrow missing-commitment warning in #1866's
productive-only control. **Limits:** the effect's intentional grounding remains
primitive, functional readings are not a theory of conscious/rational uptake,
and these are joint finite predicate assignments, *not* a metaphysically
admissible impersonal first cause. A genuine substantive impersonal account may
grant real understanding to secondary agents, not only a correct sensor.

**New positive *conditional* connection, retaining one witness.**
`TruthCore.Results.Causality.IntentionalExemplarExtension.
understandsCausedEffectsFromSelfAndExemplars` composes:

1. `IntrinsicSelfKnowledge About Correct Apprehends Grounded g`, supplied
   conditionally by draft #1866's
   `actualRootSelfKnowledgeFromWisdom` at that *same* `ActualRoot m w g`.
2. **OPEN** `RootToEsseCauseAt F m w`: translates only actual concrete
   grounding by this selected root in `m.grounds w` into `F.CausesIn` at
   `F.esse`, and carries actual existence from `m` into `F.history.ontic`.
   The two causal/grounding relations are not definitionally identical.
3. **OPEN** `EffectExemplarCoverage`: for each actual effect `x` of this
   `g` in this shared frame/world and a **correct** about-`g` self-content,
   an exemplar specification identifies
   a *correct content about x*. This is an ontological/intelligible specification,
   not automatically an act of thought. Causation does not imply it.
4. **OPEN** `CognitivelyTakesUpExemplar`: this same g's grounded apprehension
   of the self-content genuinely extends to the specified content. A rule for
   emitting a true proposition does not provide this bridge.

The Lean conclusion is actual grounded apprehension by **g** of correct
content about each **existing actual x with `F.CausesIn g x F.esse`**.
The additional `necessaryRootUnderstandsGroundedEffects` theorem returns
**the same** `ActualRoot m w g` and `NecessaryAt m.ontic w g`, plus
knowledge of the actually grounded concrete effects at this world, provided
the OPEN relation/actuality adapter is accepted. There is no knowledge of all
possible effects or of actual uncaused beings, and no assumption that a
productive power is itself intentional. The proof takes one content witness
for self and explicitly constructs another for each effect; under the
separate single-referent `About` interpretation,
`otherBeingNeedsOtherContent` shows the contents must be distinct when
`g ≠ x`. This avoids equivocating between knowledge of an intrinsic source
form, a true specification of an effect and knowledge of the effect itself.

**Independent omission assignments.** The same selected actual root,
its modal necessity, ground-to-CAUSE_2 calibration, coherent temporal
history and actual cognitive secondary effect remain populated: without
exemplar coverage there is no effect-knowledge, and with true specifications
but no cognitive uptake there is still no effect-knowledge. With both bridges
there is grounded knowledge of the effect at the same source. This is a
finite non-entailment/control for the stated syntax, not an objection that
fully specified divine exemplary knowledge is incoherent.

**Toward will, without manufacturing choice.**
`possessedGoodRestFromSameSource` specializes the existing
`PossessionSensitiveAppetite` model of q.19: for that same g,
`OwnGood(g)` plus its actual self-knowledge and the **OPEN**
`SelfKnownGoodIsRepresented` bridge make the possessed good represented.
The existing **OPEN** `Calibration` and `Availability` give an available,
positively ranked `retain` response, not the seeking of a missing good.
Neither this response nor `IntrinsicSelfKnowledge` entails the old `HasWill`
without the independently open recognition of intellectual appetite. The
already certified `LocalTeleologyGodIntellect.godHasWill` consumes
`WillFollowsIntellectReading` and any supported `GodHasIntellect`, with a
separate GOD_4 existence witness. Actual willing, selected outcomes, free
creation and willing others' good require additional content-sensitive
adoption and selection premises; a pure act does not automatically output
every possible effect.

**Cumulative graph and competing support.** Current conditional spine:
`ActualRoot m w g` + `NecessaryAt m.ontic w g` + subsistent esse + pure wisdom +
**`PureAct InPotency g`** + #1866's three OPEN self-understanding bridges → `IntrinsicSelfKnowledge(g)` →
[OPEN `RootToEsseCauseAt F m w` + OPEN correct-self-content exemplar coverage +
OPEN cognitive uptake] →
`UnderstandsActualEffects(g)`; in parallel
`IntrinsicSelfKnowledge(g)` + `OwnGood(g)` +
[OPEN known-good representation, calibration, availability] →
`RestCapacity(g)`. The unchanged
`29818edd` GodHasIntellect capacity endpoint also needs its explicit
recognition; then `090be689` WillFollowsIntellect feeds `66aecdd7`
GodHasWill, and GOD_4 existence `8626f8a5` is separately required.
This is a connected *research plan* and code-level conditional, **not**
a newly registered support route.

The immateriality/q.14 route takes an altogether-immaterial subject to
intellect via `ImmaterialIsIntellectualReading`; local teleology needs an
intellectual governor *identical to* the first-mover/GOD_4 maximum; moral
governance needs the source's *own* practical understanding and end-adoption,
rather than a norm's intelligibility. All differ from exemplar-intentional
uptake and retain their shared source-identification and substantive
intellectual-recognition burdens. The first source's causal production
of intelligent creatures counts once, not as independent evidence for each
route. Compare the strongest positive impersonal alternative from
`intentionality-943.md`, `form-selection-1380.md`,
`mind-physicalism-fork-419.md`: an underived nonrepresenting source with
law-governed secondary beings exhibiting content-sensitive learning and
actual cognition. It supplies a positive local explanation; it does not by
itself establish or deny intrinsic pure wisdom of the ultimate source.

**Overall provisional assessment (initial independent challenge repaired;
final-head re-review pending).**
This strengthens the exact diagnostic comparison because the produced

Downward revision follows from an equally substantive account of that
pure perfection in a wholly impersonal source, or a successful challenge to
the wisdom-as-pure-perfection / cognitive-basing readings. A model assigning
the same outputs under both readings cannot decide the issue.

## Immanent primary object and subsistent self-presence (2026-11-23, continuation of #1855/#1866/#1873)

**Question and status.** What is the strongest *positive* reason that the same subsistent, pure-act first source actually understands itself, not merely that it can make another understand? This checkpoint separates the selected **immanent act** branch from #1866's wisdom-to-content-capacity branch and #1873's epistemic exemplar-uptake branch. New Lean declarations are analyst frameworks, not newly attributed historical claims, registered corpus arguments, or proof of a premise's applicability.

### Two different starting points and a strongest positive reconstruction

1. **Whole-being wisdom (existing q.4 route).** `OwnBeing g` + `OwnBeingLacksNoPerfectionOfBeing` + `PurePerfectionsPertainToBeing` + `Pure wisdom` give `Has g wisdom`. This has independent force against the notion that a *maximal source* must have wisdom only because it causes an intellectual creature. But it does not distinguish intrinsic intellectual excellence from virtual capacity to produce cognition until the sense of the purported pure perfection is specified. Classify *understanding as actually grasping truth* as a pure perfection: what is positive is the act's realization of truth, not the limitations of learning, discursive succession, change, fallibility, representational tokens, or sensory organs. Excluding these modes does not subtract the positive intelligible act. This is a **reasoned candidate interpretation**, not an automatic consequence of the generic `Has` predicate; the substantive rival can deny that intellectual act is a feature every maximally perfect being must instantiate.
2. **Independent immateriality route (existing q.14 a.1).** The old `ImmaterialIsIntellectualReading` supplies `HasIntellect g` when the source is both non-composite of matter/form and a component of nothing. Aquinas's reason is that intellectual cognition involves possession of a form *without the material restrictions* of that form's concrete instantiation. This supports a source-level aptitude, rather than inference backward from an effect's intelligence; the inferential leap from ultimate immateriality to genuine intellectual aptitude remains the **OPEN** q.14 a.1 premise. Mere negation of matter by itself is not a proof of such aptitude (cf. `FormReception.oldTargetOmission`).
3. **Actual exercise, not a sleeping faculty.** Reuse `IntellectIsCapacityToUnderstand` (POTENCY_3, `CanBe`) and `UnderstandingCapacityExercisedOrInPotency` (the explicit POTENCY_3 → POTENCY_2 bridge at `understanding`). Given `PureAct InPotency g` in POTENCY_2, the disjunction cannot terminate in unactualized receptive potency: `InAct g understanding`. This is the act/potency mechanism of `intellectualPureActOperatesFromCapacity`, now taken at the actual-understanding respect. It requires `InAct` to denote **formal intellectual exercise**, not a virtual productive-power reading. Under POTENCY_1, this capacity/exercise step has a different interpretation; the translation to content-indexed capacity remains open, as #1866 documents.
4. **Why *self* is the primary object.** An actual immanent intellectual operation has an immediate intelligible species/form (`ImmanentActHasSpecies`); under subsistent esse and absence of inhering accidental perfection, a distinct primary form cannot inform the source (`SubsistentActHasOnlyOwnSpecies`). The source's own intelligible essence is objectively about *that source* (`OwnFormPresentsSelf`). If the act grasps the **primary object** of its immediate form (`ActApprehendsPrimaryObject`), the very same `g` apprehends `g`. These assumptions express the distinct content of ST I q.14 a.2 and a.4; they are not a re-labelled `WisdomSelfCapacity` or a prior self-knowledge conclusion. The source's essence is not a *separate* inner picture; `S` may be the form's type, with `ownForm g` itself furnishing the primary intelligible species. The proof works without a temporal reception, a second entity representing God, or an infinite chain of acts thinking about previous acts.
5. **Intrinsic grounding and scope.** `GroundedInOwnForm` is a stipulated **selected interpretation** of the prior `Grounded` parameter: actual apprehension rests in the knower's *own* immediate intelligible form and its directed object. It does not assert that all true representations are understanding or prove equality with every possible `CognitivelyGrounded` relation. `intrinsicSelfKnowledgeFromImmanentPrimaryForm` yields an instance of #1866's `IntrinsicSelfKnowledge` at the **same g**, with `About c g := c = g` and `Correct c g := c = g` (single primary-object content; other-content representations need not be identity). The four primary-object inputs remain OPEN and must be defended together rather than judged from their names.

**The substantial classical inference.** If a first source is fully and intrinsically intelligible *and* is already an actual intellectual operation, then its self-presence and simplicity explain why it does not await a further representation to know itself. An external producer/consumer feedback loop may track facts and make rational secondary beings; that does not make the producer the subject of *their* acts. The relevant difference is that an immanent operation belongs to its subject, whereas efficient production terminates in an effect outside it. Pure act removes a merely latent **immanent** capacity; it does not make every productive possibility an actual external effect. This is the strongest currently reconstructible case, not an unconditional proof that all immaterial sources think.

### Typed graph and source identity

```text
same actual/necessary first source g  [independent actual-root and necessity witness]
  ├─ OwnBeing g + pure wisdom + whole-being perfection → Has g wisdom
  │    └─ [OPEN WisdomSelfCapacity; #1866 exercise/grounding] → SelfKnowledge(g)
  └─ material/component-free g + [OPEN q14a1 grade] → HasIntellect g
       + [OPEN POTENCY_3 capacity, POTENCY_3→POTENCY_2 exercise] + PureAct(g)
       → actually understands at the understanding respect
       + [OPEN immanent species exists; no separate primary species;
                own form presents self; act grasps primary object]
       → IntrinsicSelfKnowledge(g)  [grounding = own immediate species]
              ├─ [OPEN grounding-meaning recognition] → independent Grounded profile
              ├─ [OPEN primary self → exemplar specification + cognitive uptake,
              │        and selected-root → CAUSE_2/esse adapter] → knows actual effects
              └─ [OPEN HAS_INTELLECT_1 recognition, if needed] → GodHasIntellect(g)
                       + [OPEN WillFollowsIntellectReading] → GodHasWill(g)
```

The second branch **already** uses the same old `ImmaterialIsIntellectualReading` as the registered `GodHasIntellect` consumer; do not register a duplicate universal `GodHasIntellect` argument. The new result supplies a *stronger actual-self-knowledge conclusion* under additional, independently identifiable content conditions. An actual-root application supplies the existing `ActualRoot m w g` / `NecessaryAt m.ontic w g` and its existing root-to-OwnBeing, root-to-pure-act and noncomposition connections for **the same g**; the new theorem does **not** create those witnesses. Stage-two `GodHasWill` still consumes capacity-level intellect and the separate will-follows-intellect premise; neither actual choice nor free contingent creation follows.

### Controls, surviving impersonal account and comparative judgment

The new Lean-only `immanentSelfPresenceControls` gives three Bool configurations using the earlier source/effect frame where appropriate:

- **Impersonal producer:** source `true` is OwnBeing/pure act and causes secondary `false`; the secondary being genuinely apprehends a correct self-content. The secondary's act/capacity/primary-form principles hold; the same source has no intellectual act. The source-level q.14 a.1 immaterial-to-intellect application fails. This is a structural **non-entailment** and shows where the positive classical route discriminates, not that the first source could metaphysically be this model.
- **Actual intelligence, alien primary object:** even granting source-level intellect, actual intellectual exercise and an immediate species equal to own form, assigning that form only an *other* object's primary aboutness prevents self-knowledge. It isolates `OwnFormPresentsSelf`; a theorist accepting subsistent truth/intelligibility may reject such alien aboutness on philosophical grounds, and the Boolean assignment alone cannot decide.
- **Nonempty positive case:** highest immateriality, ability, pure actuality, own immediate species, self-directed primary aboutness and genuine apprehension jointly give actual self-knowledge. No distinct representation is part of the formal profile.

**Law-governed impersonal ground.** One simple necessary source has intelligible stable powers and laws; secondary physical/functional producer–consumer mechanisms and genuinely rational agents explain accurate information tracking, local cognitive norms and counterfactual performance. This account distinguishes the source of cognitive systems from the subject of their operations. At the *first-source* level, it contests either (a) the q.14 a.1 transition from supreme immateriality to source-level intellectual aptitude, (b) understanding as a pure perfection of being, or (c) the applicability of subsistent self-form to an immediate cognitive act. None of those denials is established merely by showing how cognition can occur downstream.

**Stronger supra-noetic objection: Plotinus's One.** The earlier primary-text analysis in [immaterial intellect reception](../immaterial-intellect-reception.md#the-strongest-simple-cognition-reply-and-the-remaining-disagreement) and [Plotinian composition/unity](plotinian-composition-unity.md) offers a positive competing metaphysical picture, not a merely hypothetical false `HasIntellect` label. The first is ungenerated, simple, self-sufficient and causally prior to determinate Intellect. Its derivative Nous possesses articulated intellectual forms; the One does not perform ordinary noesis, because subject/object articulation threatens the first's absolute unity (VI.9.2, .6). **Do not describe it as wholly unaware:** V.4.2 invokes qualified self-discrimination and *as-if* awareness, while VI.9.6 denies ordinary noesis without calling the first ignorant. These passages demand an interpretive reconciliation rather than a forced binary of mind versus brute matter.

The sharp disagreement is whether even *one* intrinsically self-directed `PrimaryAbout (ownForm g) g` plus its `ActApprehendsPrimaryObject` realization amounts to real internal differentiation, or only to distinct intentional descriptions of the **same** uncomposed act. Aquinas (ST I q.14 a.2/a.4) denies that an immanent intellect identical to its form and act introduces a second constitutive entity; the Plotinian argument challenges whether identity alone removes plurality of *intelligible articulation*. The finite `alienPrimaryObject` assignment does not model that ontological dispute; it only separates the typed aboutness input. A stronger discriminating test should formalize a condition on cognitive articulation with two forks: (i) articulation implies a genuinely real multiplicity incompatible with strict simplicity; (ii) subject/object directedness can be a non-constitutive intentional distinction in one simple act. Neither fork is presently established, and the two first-source accounts may differ over whether the One even inhabits the common `Being`/GOD_4 domain. Their positive resources are incomparable without a domain-and-sense bridge.

**Existing form-result reuse check.** `Round5Worklist.UnderstandingIsThroughIntelligibleForm` (registered statement `73b3d83a`) gives some intelligible form **after** a subject already understands some object. It does not identify the *immediate* form of an intellectual act, or transfer from the `InAct` understanding-respect predicate to `Understands x q`. Consequently `ImmanentActHasSpecies` is deliberately a stronger, explicitly OPEN premise with its own burden; silently substituting the old result would presuppose both adapters. The new theorem does not create a second registered source claim or duplicate `intellectFormFromUnderstanding`.

A direct impersonal model and the Plotinian supra-noetic first must not be conflated: the former explains secondary cognition via laws and mechanisms; the latter gives a distinct priority and unity account and can acknowledge qualified awareness. Structural omission models are not evidence that classical premises are false.

**Provisional assessment (2026-11-23).** The self-form account is a substantively stronger **positive route** to *actual intrinsic self-understanding* than an argument from causing intelligent effects, because it explains intentional subject, primary object and nonrepresentational presence in one immanent act. Combined with the independently used q.14 immateriality route and whole-perfection wisdom interpretation, it offers cumulative support, **but it does not discharge** any of their metaphysical bridge premises. The leading live test is whether pure *intelligibility* of subsistent actuality plus the already supported immateriality/operation arguments truly mandates **source-level** intentional grasp, rather than permitting primary self-form to be objectively intelligible but wholly unapprehended. The Plotinian challenge makes this sharper: does intentional self-presence introduce **real articulation** incompatible with absolute simplicity, or is the distinction only intentional, with no additional form or act? The existing q.14 a.2/a.4 positive reading and Plotinian V.4.2/VI.9.2, .6 primary evidence justify this targeted comparison, not a claim that either side has been defeated. A positive account of self-intelligibility independent of cognition would weaken the proposed self-form bridge; an argument that genuine nonreceptive understanding is an unqualified perfection and that the source already instantiates it would strengthen it. The separate exemplar/actual-effects step remains live: understanding one's power does not alone identify which contingent effects actually obtain without a content-sensitive bridge.

**Verification boundary.** No corpus source claim, root-route statement, result registration, closed premise, or computed support edge is altered; the formal increment is an independently checkable conditional theorem plus structural controls. The existing #1866/#1873 results, teleology and will consumers remain unchanged. The exact CI `frontier-diff` report, not this expected-no-change statement, determines any computed delta.

## C4: intrinsic objective exemplarity versus understanding (2026-11-23)

**Question.** Does the *same necessary first source* intrinsically exercise intentional understanding because it eminently contains intellectual perfection? This is C4, not the earlier claim that an efficient cause can produce intelligent effects. The Lean-only ObjectiveEminence extension of IntentionalExemplarExtension and its result module test a stronger impersonal interpretation alongside an actual self-knower with at most one real constituent. This continues merged #1855, #1866, #1873 and #1879 without changing their registered arguments. [Assessment record](../../corpus/attempts/58750abd-e037-4902-b2f5-7c83e0f467a3.json)

### The distinctions C4 must preserve

| Role | What belongs to the first source? | Consequence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Productive intellectual power | Ability to make a distinct, actually understanding secondary agent | Intelligence of the effect alone |
| Formally intrinsic aptitude | Its own capacity for understanding | Source-level capacity, not actual exercise |
| Objective/exemplary eminence | Its own intelligible form, objective self-intelligibility and normative relation to an actually cognizing effect | An intrinsic intelligible exemplar, but not yet subject-level uptake |
| Immanent cognitive capacity | Ability itself to apprehend content about itself | #1866 wisdom-to-self-capacity bridge, still OPEN |
| Actual intentional understanding | This very source truly apprehends content by its own intelligible form | Content-indexed intrinsic self-knowledge, not mere correct representation or signal production |
| Will and choice | The source's own intelligent appetite, exercise and selection | Independent further bridges; cognition does not by logic entail free creation |

The old causal-preexistence and CartesianAdequacy propositions over GradesFrame use IN_ACT_4 with formal/eminent possession left open. #1855 isolates formal possession by the intellectual effect and formal or virtual possession by its source. The definition of virtual possession still permits productive-power and higher intrinsic senses. **IntrinsicExemplarEminence is a new analyst comparison at a stronger *objective* sense**, not a definition of IN_ACT_4, InActVirtually, HasIntellect or noesis. Treating this sense as the whole pure perfection of *subjective* understanding would itself require an OPEN semantic/metaphysical argument.

The strongest defensible causal-adequacy proposal is not that anyone who produces thinkers is thinking. It is that an adequate first source cannot be wholly without a *pure* excellence positively present in its effect. For intellect this requires two further philosophical claims: understanding truth, abstracted from finite reception, discursive motion, error and bodily mediation, is an irreducible pure perfection; and higher-mode possession of that perfection cannot be reduced to the objective norm of a different knower's act. This is the substantive missing premise. The effect's own cognitive operation may belong properly to the secondary subject even when the first source adequately grounds the effect's reality.

### Strong impersonal interpretation (¬C4)

The existing two-world, two-being Control.frame and Control.rootProfile supply an actual source **true** and distinct effect **false**. The source is an ActualRoot at the selected world, NecessaryAt, OwnBeing, pure act in POTENCY_2, and simple in the chosen real-constituent respect. Pure act is nonvacuous in these controls: the control-specific potency predicate holds of the contingent effect only, so the effect is not pure act and every pure-act being existing at the selected world is NecessaryAt there (the shape of FirstMoverNecessity.PureActBeingsNecessaryAt). The earlier draft inherited the constantly-false WisdomSelfUnderstanding noPotency predicate, under which the contingent effect would also have counted as pure act; that vacuity is repaired. The same CAUSE_2-at-esse edge produces the actual effect. The effect actually and correctly apprehends its own true content with its own cognitive grounding, rather than merely carrying a labelled HasIntellect predicate.

The source is assigned its own intelligible form, that form is primarily about the source (the same PrimaryAbout relation and own-form/primary-object control witnesses as #1879's OwnFormPresentsSelf, reused rather than restated), and a source-borne Norms relation links the form to the caused subject's **actual correct** self-directed content. Yet the source does not itself Apprehend any content. This is Control.ImpersonalEminence. It goes beyond the older mere productive-power omission, and beyond correct external signals: the secondary effect actually knows, and the source is assigned the modelled own-form, self-aboutness and Norms relations, which are *interpreted* as objective intelligent-exemplar structure. Control.ProductiveWithoutExemplarity holds the genuine cognizer fixed but removes source-level own-form exemplarity, making the difference explicit. The theorem exemplarEminenceProducesKnower only projects the caused-knower clause; it does **not** derive source-level cognition. The new eminentFormApprehendedIfActual, an instantiation (transcription) of #1879's OPEN ActApprehendsPrimaryObject rule at the eminence witness, shows where the gap lies. The source apprehends itself, the primary object of its own form (#1879's object-level ApprehendsObject relation), **provided** it is actually in the understanding act with its own form as immediate species. Objective eminence supplies neither input. Reaching C4's content-indexed IntrinsicSelfKnowledge additionally needs #1879's content reading (C := B, About/Correct as identity, GroundedInOwnForm) and the open rule itself.

**Scope of the countermodels.** HasOwnForm, PrimaryAbout and Norms are unconstrained primitives, Correct is not defined from Norms, and ProductiveWithoutExemplarity differs from the impersonal model only by setting HasOwnForm to False; so each countermodel's content is a non-entailment within this vocabulary, not a model of what objective exemplarity metaphysically is.

**Semantic limit.** Norms is an explicitly interpreted primitive, instantiated rather than explained by a Boolean rule. The model is a *structural* joint assignment, not a metaphysical proof that objective exemplarity suffices to ground truth or warrants the entire perfection of intellect. If the intended stronger sense of genuine eminent **intellection** already includes intrinsic uptake of its intelligible object, this model does not satisfy that reading; a rival must argue for an objective-exemplar notion of excellence that is not covert subjective cognition. This is precisely what it means to test the stronger meaning instead of switching back to mere productivity.

A viable positive account of the impersonal alternative says the necessary source's own structure and powers explain regular, counterfactually robust informational relations; genuine secondary agents possess proper acts of cognition and norms; the source is the ultimate exemplar/truth-ground, not the subject of those acts. The classical counterargument is an asymmetry challenge: why should grasping truth be a positive pure perfection present in derivative minds but absent even analogically as an immanent act in an unlimited source? The impersonal reply distinguishes a grounding excellence from every proper operation of a grounded species. Both alternatives owe an argument at this exact level; the finite omission settles neither.

### Simple self-understanding and the Plotinian objection

The source-backed Plotinian One is causally prior to articulated Nous and resists ordinary noesis as intelligible multiplicity, while qualified self-presence remains in its source discussion. Do not caricature it as a completely unknowing physical mechanism, nor silently identify a source beyond *ousia* with the same subsistent-esse GOD_4 carrier. That remains an OPEN domain and interpretation bridge.

The new SourceNoesisRequiresRealPlurality represents one concrete, strong fork: intrinsic self-understanding entails **two distinct real intrinsic constituents**. ConstitutivelySimple contradicts that stronger thesis if the source knows itself; Lean theorem strictUnityExcludesNoesisUnderRealDuality formalizes the conditional. The opposing Control.SimpleIntentionalEminence retains the very same necessary, purely actual exemplar source and actual secondary cognition, grants the source actual self-apprehension, but has only one real constituent; the model shows the real-duality principle is not entailed by the other modelled conditions (RealConstituent is linked to no other relation); it is an extra premise the Plotinian argument must supply; the model neither refutes it nor represents subject/act/object identity.

**Deliberate transposition and scope.** SourceNoesisRequiresRealPlurality formalizes only a *constituent* sub-reading of the Plotinian objection and deliberately transposes it onto OwnBeing bearers, the GOD_4-side carrier, without asserting that the One belongs to that domain. The earlier primary-text finding in [immaterial intellect reception](../immaterial-intellect-reception.md#the-strongest-simple-cognition-reply-and-the-remaining-disagreement) already records that the strongest Plotinian objection "survives merely removing two numerically different relata", so this fork is not that objection. #1879's two-fork articulation test above (articulation as real multiplicity versus non-constitutive intentional distinction in one simple act) is still **not formalized**.

**Unresolved stronger Plotinian claim:** intelligible articulation might be real/metaphysically multiple *without* requiring distinct constituents. Our counterassignment shows the independence of only the selected constitutive reading. It is not a defeat of that broader explanatory objection. The central question is whether intentional directedness is a genuinely distinct relation or an intrinsic self-presence of one act, and whether the first's objective intelligibility mandates source-level realization of that positive intentional excellence.

### Same-source C1–C4 combinations

| Conditional package at one common source g | Licensed conclusion |
| --- | --- |
| C1–C2 existence/necessity route and root witness, without C4 | A necessary foundation, cognition underdetermined |
| C1–C3 with source pure act plus genuinely intellectual effects, on the objective-exemplar reading | The controls give a joint assignment with source-level non-apprehension (a non-entailment in this vocabulary, not a metaphysical coherence result). That pure act rules out only dormant **immanent** potency, not nonactualized productive effects, is an interpretation of POTENCY_2, not something the model licenses |
| OwnBeing g + whole-being perfection + Pure wisdom | Has g wisdom by the verified conditional route, but the mode of possession and own intellect remain OPEN |
| C1–C3 + source-level immanent capacity + #1866 capacity/exercise, pure act and grounding principles | Actual intrinsic understanding of **g** under those OPEN premises |
| C1–C3 + q.14 immateriality-to-intellect + POTENCY_3 to POTENCY_2 exercise + pure act + #1879 immediate self-form/primary-object principles | Actual self-understanding of **g**, with explicit interpretation bridges |
| C4 directly assumed or cumulatively supported + a genuinely eminent first source in its exact chosen sense | That same source understands; C4 alone supplies no bearer and cannot be substituted for C1–C3 |
| Same-source actual understanding + #1873 exemplar coverage, cognitive uptake and root-to-CAUSE_2 adapter | Knowledge of specifically caused actual effects; not unrestricted omniscience |
| Source-level intellectual capacity + independent WillFollowsIntellectReading | Capacity-level GodHasWill only, not actual willing, choice or free contingent creation |

An actual-root witness and NecessaryAt fact hold in the controls, not an arbitrary C1–C3 modal theorem. Formal necessity, intrinsic capacity, actual cognition, willing, and the GOD_4 identity/naming consumer are distinct. These models do not prove any C1–C3 metaphysical premise; their purpose is to compare interpretations while retaining the same nonempty existential source and actual effect.

**Cross-family support, provisional.** The best positive case is stronger than a productive cause argument: SCG I.28/q.4 offers the whole-being rationale for *intrinsic* wisdom; q.14 immateriality supplies intrinsic intellectual aptitude; pure act combined with an immanent capacity/exercise bridge excludes a merely latent receptive capacity; #1879 explains **whose** actual act grasps **what** primary object without a distinct mental token. Fifth Way intelligent governance and moral-governance routes provide distinct conditional support for a director, but they cannot silently transport effect-level cognitive behavior to this same first source. Shared source identity and the relevant scope/intentional premises remain OPEN. Direct warranted acceptance of C4 may coexist with these cumulative routes.

The objective impersonal rival is substantive but not automatically warranted: the formal Norms assignment does not demonstrate that genuinely subjective intellectual perfection is absent from the metaphysical fullness of its first source. Conversely the positive route does not yet establish that objective source-level intelligibility *must* be exercised as immanent knowing. No warranted unconditional metaphysical winner has been established by this batch.

**Next discriminator and revision condition.** Defend or reject the **pure-perfection criterion for actual intentional understanding** in a source already granting objective first-exemplar excellence. Test whether non-constitutive intentional distinctions can answer the stronger Plotinian articulated-intellect argument, not only the real-constituent fork. A demonstrated source-specific objective norm with no pure act of truth-grasp weakens the C4 bridge; a substantive account of subject-level grasp as an unrestricted immanent pure act strengthens it. Neither is decided by predicate naming.

**Verification boundary and self-sweep.** The code adds three unregistered helper theorems (a projection, the same-witness link to #1879's ActApprehendsPrimaryObject, and the real-duality conditional) and finite joint/omission controls. No registered corpus arguments, statements, attributed claims, bindings, GOD_4 edges or root-premise closure change; the expected computed frontier-diff is **none**, subject to final-head CI. The selected source g is identical through actual root, necessity, pure act, first-cause edge, intrinsic source form, norm and (in the positive model) apprehension. The caused effect is distinct and actually apprehends correct content. Norms does not entail source-level Apprehends, and the separate real-constituent carrier is not collapsed into content or formal essence. The duality premise is one selected interpretation, not all of Plotinus.

**Semantic challenge outcome (2026-11-23).** An independent semantic challenge of head `b4ca11060` returned *mergeable after listed repairs*. It accepted exemplarEminenceProducesKnower as an honest projection; strictUnityExcludesNoesisUnderRealDuality as valid, nonvacuous and with every premise listed; one source throughout; no smuggling or equivocation; and the impersonal-rival prose as fair. Repairs applied in PR #1892: the P1 overstatement that the simple model "captures" Thomistic subject/act/object identity is withdrawn (the model shows only non-entailment of the real-duality principle); pure act is made nonvacuous with a control-specific potency predicate; the countermodels are stated as non-entailments within an unconstrained own-form/Norms vocabulary; the Norms clause is scoped to correct self-directed contents; carrier and at-most-one wording corrected; the Plotinian transposition, the earlier "survives merely removing two numerically different relata" finding and the unformalized #1879 articulation test are recorded; duplicated control vocabulary is replaced by the #1873/#1879 controls and the PrimaryAbout relation. A targeted re-check of the repairs (head `e3d64aafd`) confirmed the P1 and all nine P2s resolved. It raised one P1 and two P2s about the new helper theorem: it apprehends the source (via its form), it is a transcription of #1879's rule, and the remaining gap includes #1879's content reading. All three were corrected in the coordinator's follow-up commit.
